680	
681	## InitParams implementation (0.44.0)
682	
683	Single-step cycle: introduce `Context` + `InitParams` and
684	port `init` to the new shape. Establishes the worked
685	example for the design captured in
686	[Ops layer architecture (forward-looking)](#ops-layer-architecture-forward-looking)
687	above; remaining subcommands defer to later cycles.
688	
689	### Naming differs from "Ops layer architecture" section above
690	
691	Written before this cycle, the design section uses
692	`Workspace` and `XOptions`. This cycle adopts:
693	
694	- `Workspace` → `Context`. Cargo owns "workspace"
695	  formally, and this codebase already uses "workspace"
696	  for the dual-repo project root (`find_workspace_root`).
697	  Doubly overloaded.
698	- `XOptions` → `XParams`. Avoids collision with Rust's
699	  `Option<T>` (`InitParams` fields are a mix of required
700	  and `Option<T>`-typed, which `Options` muddles).
701	  Doesn't collide with `src/options_flags/` either.
702	
703	The forward-looking design section is left as-is for
704	this cycle; once 0.44.0 lands and the worked example
705	is in tree, that section can be retitled / updated
706	during a future close-out so the codebase has one
707	naming.
708	
709	### Why init
710	
711	`args.account.account` ergonomics — the trigger for the
712	whole design — lives here; init also has the deepest
713	leaf nesting (account / repo / scope / private /
714	template / push retry). If the pattern survives init,
715	sync / chid / push / clone port trivially.
716	
717	### In scope
718	
719	- `src/context.rs` (new): `Context` struct holding the
720	  workspace root path + loaded `UserConfig`. Built once
721	  at startup.
722	- `src/init/params.rs` (new): `InitParams` flat plain
723	  struct (owns its values) + `impl From<&InitArgs> for
724	  InitParams`.
725	- `src/init.rs`: port `plan_init` and any other
726	  init-side `pub fn` entrypoints to
727	  `fn (&Context, &InitParams)`. Body reads
728	  `params.account` instead of `args.account.account`.
729	- `src/main.rs`: build `Context` once at startup; the
730	  init dispatch arm passes `(&ctx, &(&args).into())`.
731	- `src/init/tests.rs`: pure-unit tests construct
732	  `Context` + `InitParams` directly (no clap
733	  `try_parse_from` plumbing); integration paths still
734	  exercise the clap edge.
735	
736	### Out of scope (deferred)
737	
738	- Sweep across sync / chid / push / clone / etc.
739	  Each is its own later cycle.
740	- Per chores-09 "five rules": typed errors, returned
741	  outcomes (vs `println!`), `ProgressSink`. Keep this
742	  cycle to the structural split only.
743	- `Context` fields beyond workspace root + user
744	  config. Defer until a real consumer surfaces.
745	
746	### Pre-commit checklist
747	
748	Standard: `cargo fmt`, `cargo clippy --all-targets --
749	-D warnings`, `cargo test`, `cargo install --path .
750	--locked`, retest.
751	
752	### Edits
753	
754	- `Cargo.toml`: bump 0.43.0 → 0.44.0.
755	- `src/context.rs` (new): `Context` struct holding
756	  `user_config: UserConfig` + `Context::load()` ctor;
757	  declared `mod context;` in `src/main.rs`.
758	- `src/init/params.rs` (new): `InitParams` flat struct
759	  + `impl From<&InitArgs> for InitParams` (production
760	  default sets `create_symlink: true`); declared
761	  `mod params; pub use params::InitParams;` in
762	  `src/init.rs`.
763	- `src/init.rs`: `pub fn init` signature changed
764	  from `init(args: &InitArgs, create_symlink: bool)`
765	  to `init(ctx: &Context, params: &InitParams)`
766	  (`create_symlink` folded into `InitParams`).
767	  Internal helpers (`plan_init`, `plan_from_url`,
768	  `plan_from_path`, `plan_from_bare_name`,
769	  `plan_remote`, `plan_local`, `build_plan`,
770	  `create_por`, `create_dual`, `push_repo`,
771	  `run_remote_step`) all switched from
772	  `args: &InitArgs` to `params: &InitParams`. Body
773	  reads `params.account` instead of
774	  `args.account.account` (and similarly across all
775	  leaf nestings). `args.config.resolve(ConfigKind::None)`
776	  call sites collapse to `&params.config` since the
777	  resolve runs at the boundary now. `cfg` inside
778	  `pub fn init` switched from `config::load()?` to
779	  `&ctx.user_config`.
780	- `src/main.rs`: `mod context;` added; init dispatch
781	  builds `Context::load()` then converts
782	  `InitParams::from(&init_args)` and calls
783	  `init::init(&ctx, &params)`.
784	- `src/test_helpers.rs`: `Fixture` and `FixturePor`
785	  builders updated — load `Context`, build
786	  `InitParams::from(&args)`, override
787	  `params.create_symlink = false`, then call
788	  `init(&ctx, &params)`. Imports updated to bring
789	  `Context` and `InitParams` into scope.
790	- `src/init/tests.rs`: every `plan_init(&args, ...)`
791	  call site rewritten to
792	  `plan_init(&InitParams::from(&args), ...)`. The two
793	  `plan_init(&args_for(...), ...)` sites split across
794	  multiple lines for readability. Tests of `InitArgs`
795	  (parse/defaults) untouched — those exercise the
796	  clap edge.
797	- `notes/chores-09.md`: this section + the
798	  `## InitParams implementation (0.44.0)` plan
799	  section.
800	- `notes/todo.md`: drop the cycle's `## In Progress`
801	  entry; add `## Done` entry; rewrite the
802	  `Ops layer / CLI decoupling` `## Todo` item to
803	  reflect "init done, sweep remaining."
804

1	# Chores-09.md
2	
3	General chores notes — design captures (forward-looking) and
4	post-implementation chore entries. Same shape as chores-01..08.md;
5	09 starts here because chores-07 (0.42.0 cycle, 600+ lines) and
6	chores-08 (0.41.1 cycle, 1500+ lines) are both already large; the
7	init-clone-refactor rebase landing is a natural new-file
8	boundary.
9	
10	Subsection headers use the trailing-version format from CLAUDE.md
11	when they correspond to a release: `## Description (X.Y.Z)`.
12	
13	## init-clone-refactor rebase landing (0.42.0-4.7)
14	
15	Rebased main (0.42.0-0..-4.6) onto init-clone-refactor at its
16	0.41.1 close-out tip (`slvprlpw`). Chids preserved; ochid
17	trailers in the rebased commits stayed structurally valid (the
18	.claude pairings they point at had been WC-only during the icr
19	divergence; -4.7 itself re-establishes the .claude side).
20	
21	### What needed real work
22	
23	- **ttzwvpoq (0.42.0-1):** full `Scope` enum lifted wholesale
24	  from pre-rebase main; `#[allow(dead_code)]` on `is_bot_only`
25	  and `Single` / `parse_scope` since icr's bundle design keeps
26	  them dormant.
27	- **mvusyowm (0.42.0-2):** icr's bundle-flatten `InitArgs` +
28	  `ScopeOption`/`ScopeKind` already supersedes mvusyowm's
29	  parser-switch + `Option<Scope>` design; took icr wholesale
30	  across all 5 init.rs conflict regions. The cycle's narrative
31	  intent (custom `--scope` parser accepting paths) becomes
32	  dormant on the icr base; the `Scope::Single` variant survives
33	  for future use.
34	- **kyurxpnu (0.42.0-4.5):** CLAUDE.md substep references merged
35	  (lulqxovr's icr-side decomposition pointer + kyurxpnu's
36	  external `substep-protocol.md` / `jj-revsets.md` links) into
37	  a single continuation paragraph under `### Versioning`.
38	- **Reference renumbering** — cycle's `[72]`/`[73]`/`[74]`
39	  collided with icr's chores-08 anchors: `[72]→[76]`,
40	  `[73]→[77]`, `[74]→[78]`, plus self-refs in chores-07's
41	  -4.5 and -4.6 sections.
42	
43	### Precautions taken (most unnecessary in retrospect)
44	
45	- `main-2` local duplicate bookmark — never read.
46	- `gca-icr-main` common-ancestor marker — never referenced.
47	- `rslv-commit` cursor bookmark — handy as a navigation
48	  aid during cascade, not load-bearing (`@-` works).
49	- `../vc-x1-main` + `../vc-x1-icr` reference clones — useful
50	  for content lookup at specific chids; replaceable with
51	  `jj log -r <chid> --patch` against the local repo.
52	- Filesystem snapshots `vc-x1-20260505-1`, `vc-x1-20260506-1`
53	  — never restored from.
54	- `~/vc-x1-rebase-status.md` scratch file — useful for
55	  resuming across sessions, but ~200 lines was over-detailed;
56	  a 30-line cursor + decisions log would have sufficed.
57	
58	### What was load-bearing
59	
60	- `jj op restore` for local rewinds.
61	- origin's canonical pre-rebase main remaining intact until
62	  the explicit force-push at the end (the only fallback that
63	  mattered).
64	- jj's chid-preservation across rebase: ochid trailers stay
65	  valid pointers without manual fixup.
66	- Per-commit cargo cycle (fmt/clippy/test/install --locked)
67	  to verify each squash kept the chain buildable.
68	
69	### Distilled recipe for next time
70	
71	1. `jj rebase` of the divergent chain onto the new base.
72	2. Per conflicted commit: `jj new <commit>`, edit conflicts,
73	   cargo cycle, `jj squash`.
74	3. After cascade clears: cargo cycle at tip, `jj git push
75	   --bookmark main`.
76	4. `.claude` side: `vc-x1 push` to author the paired commit
77	   once main converges with origin.
78	
79	Skip the duplicate bookmarks, reference clones, and
80	filesystem snapshots unless a specific reason emerges. The
81	op-log + origin are the safety net.
82	
83	### Edits
84	
85	- `notes/chores-09.md`: this file (new).
86	- `notes/todo.md`: ladder marker flips
87	  (-4.6 (current)→(done), add -4.7 (current)); add `-4.7`
88	  Done entry referenced as `[79]`; add `[79]:` ref target;
89	  move pre-0.42.0 Done entries to `notes/done.md`.
90	- `notes/done.md`: append migrated 0.40/0.41 Done entries.
91	- `Cargo.toml`: bump 0.42.0-4.6 → 0.42.0-4.7.
92	
93	## 0.42.0 close-out
94	
95	Cycle closed at -4.7 (init-clone-refactor rebase landing)
96	rather than completing the originally planned full

191	## Test-module extraction (0.43.0)
192	
193	Multi-step cycle to extract `#[cfg(test)] mod tests` (and
194	`mod integration_tests` where present) from oversized
195	production files into sibling-submodule layout. Pure
196	mechanical reshape — no behavior change, no API change.
197	Each test still reaches private items via
198	`use super::*;`.
199	
200	### Goals
201	
202	- Shrink production files so the actual code is easier
203	  to navigate (`init.rs` is 2576 lines today, ~1093 of
204	  which are tests).
205	- Establish a layout shape that future cycles can keep
206	  using (further splitting into `cli.rs` / `ops.rs` per
207	  the ops-layer architecture, etc.).
208	- Done as one cycle so the four files end up
209	  consistent rather than half-converted across multiple
210	  cycles.
211	
212	### Per-file shape
213	
214	Non-mod.rs layout (Rust 2018+ idiom): the production
215	file keeps its top-level path; a sibling directory holds
216	children. `src/X.rs` and `src/X/` coexist — Rust resolves
217	`mod foo;` declared in `src/X.rs` to `src/X/foo.rs`.
218	
219	```
220	src/X.rs               ← production code (unchanged path)
221	src/X/
222	  tests.rs             ← moved from #[cfg(test)] mod tests
223	  integration_tests.rs ← moved from #[cfg(test)] mod
224	                         integration_tests (push, sync)
225	```
226	
227	In `src/X.rs` after extraction:
228	
229	```rust
230	// ...production code...
231	
232	#[cfg(test)]
233	mod tests;
234	
235	#[cfg(test)]
236	mod integration_tests;  // only for push, sync
237	```
238	
239	In `src/X/tests.rs`:
240	
241	```rust
242	//! Unit tests for the X module.
243	
244	use super::*;
245	// ...test fns...
246	```
247	
248	**Why non-mod.rs.** The `mod.rs` style names every
249	module's entry point identically — fuzzy finders show a
250	column of `mod.rs` rows that the reader must
251	disambiguate by directory. Multiple `tests.rs` files
252	don't have the same pain (the file is leaf content; the
253	directory tells the reader which module's tests they
254	are). Modern Rust (`cargo new`, RFC 2126) defaults to
255	non-mod.rs for exactly this reason.
256	
257	### Sub-step ladder
258	
259	Size-descending order (init has the most test bulk;
260	common is borderline):
261	
262	- 0.43.0-0 plan + version bump + this section +
263	  todo.md ladder
264	- 0.43.0-1 src/init.rs (kept) + src/init/tests.rs
265	  (~1093 test lines)
266	- 0.43.0-2 src/push.rs (kept) + src/push/{tests.rs,
267	  integration_tests.rs} (~784 test lines + integration
268	  block)
269	- 0.43.0-3 src/sync.rs (kept) + src/sync/{tests.rs,
270	  integration_tests.rs} (~600 test lines, multiple
271	  `#[cfg(test)]` blocks)
272	- 0.43.0-4 src/common.rs (kept) + src/common/tests.rs
273	  (~384 test lines, borderline — call after seeing
274	  the first three land)
275	- 0.43.0 close-out
276	
277	### Order rationale
278	
279	`init` is the worked example: largest test bulk and the
280	file most-discussed in CLAUDE.md. Getting the pattern
281	right there sets the template for the others. The bot
282	thinks `common` is worth keeping for consistency but
283	reasonable to drop after seeing the first three land —
284	its 384 lines are below the threshold where extraction
285	visibly helps.
286	
287	### Per-substep contract
288	
289	Per `notes/substep-protocol.md`:
290	
291	- Run `cargo fmt`, `cargo clippy --all-targets -- -D
292	  warnings`, `cargo test`, `cargo install --path .
293	  --locked`, retest before each sub-step commit.
294	- Bump `Cargo.toml` version at the start of each
295	  sub-step.
296	- Mark prior sub-step `(done)` and current `(current)`
297	  in todo.md ladder.
298	- Pair commits across both repos with ochid trailers.
299	
300	### Cycle exemption from per-file review
301	
302	Test-module extraction is exactly the "code moving
303	across files" refactor case CLAUDE.md
304	(`### Per-file review checkpoints > Exceptions`)
305	exempts. Each sub-step commits as one unit; no
306	checkpoint between original-file edit and new-file
307	creation.

506	## Ops layer architecture (forward-looking)
507	
508	Design target for subsequent cycles: separate clap-aware
509	parsing from subcommand operation logic so future front-ends
510	(e.g. a TUI for bot-conversation exploration, or library
511	embedding) call the same core without retrofitting. Captures
512	the conclusion of the 0.42.0-4.7 side discussion about
513	`args.account.account` ergonomics — renaming / accessor
514	shortcuts were rejected as hiding the architecture mismatch
515	rather than fixing it.
516	
517	### Goals
518	
519	- Subcommand bodies access flat fields (`opts.account`), not
520	  nested clap shapes (`args.account.account`).
521	- `src/options_flags/` leaves remain the single source of
522	  truth for flag definition (parser, help, completer).
523	- Per-subcommand `-h` stays bundle-specific.
524	- Tab completion (`vc-x1 init <TAB>`, `--account=<TAB>`)
525	  keeps working via clap_complete + dynamic completers.
526	- The ops layer is callable without clap as a dependency
527	  (today's `plan_init(args: &InitArgs, ...)` is not).
528	
529	### Two layers, three structures per subcommand
530	
531	- **CLI layer (clap-aware):** `InitArgs` / `CloneArgs` / etc.
532	  `#[derive(Args)]` types that flatten leaves from
533	  `src/options_flags/`. Own clap metadata for `-h` and
534	  completion. Live at the binary edge.
535	- **Ops layer (clap-free):** `InitOptions` / `CloneOptions`
536	  — plain structs, flat fields, `Default`. Plus `Workspace`,
537	  the shared platform handle (workspace root, loaded
538	  `UserConfig`, optional progress sink). Entrypoints are
539	  `ops::init(ws: &Workspace, opts: &InitOptions) -> Result<InitOutcome, InitError>`.
540	- **Boundary conversion:** `From<&InitArgs> for InitOptions`
541	  — one contained site per subcommand where leaf nesting is
542	  unpacked. Op bodies never see `args.xxx.xxx`.
543	
544	### `Workspace` ("context") vs per-op options
545	
546	- **`Workspace`:** shared platform every op runs against —
547	  workspace root, loaded user config, optional progress
548	  sink. Same shape across subcommands.
549	- **`InitOptions`** etc.: per-op input; shape differs by
550	  subcommand.
551	
552	Two parameters, not a single merged "god context." The
553	signature `fn init(ws: &Workspace, opts: &InitOptions)`
554	documents what the op depends on; a merged `Context`
555	containing every possible field would let any op silently
556	read any field with no signature-level visibility.
557	
558	If `Workspace` itself grows, escape valves (defer until
559	needed):
560	
561	- Split into `Workspace` (paths + config, read-only, cheap)
562	  vs `Session` (progress / cancellation, mutable). Most ops
563	  want `&Workspace`; long-running ops also accept
564	  `&Session`.
565	- Trait-based DI (`fn init<W: HasConfig + HasFs>(...)`) for
566	  multiple front-ends with genuinely different platform
567	  surfaces. Heavy in Rust; not the default.
568	
569	### Five rules for the ops layer
570	
571	1. **Plain options, flat fields, `Default`.** No clap types
572	   in `InitOptions`. Domain values like `RepoSelector` are
573	   fine (they're domain types); leaf wrappers like
574	   `RepoOption` are not.
575	2. **Typed errors.** `enum InitError` (likely via
576	   `thiserror`), not `Box<dyn Error>`. A TUI matches
577	   variants to pick dialogs; CLI formats them.
578	   `Box<dyn Error>` discards exactly the information a GUI
579	   most needs.
580	3. **Returned outcomes, not `println!`.** Each op returns a
581	   structured result; CLI formats it for stdout, TUI
582	   populates panels. Library writes nothing to stdout
583	   itself.
584	4. **Progress via a sink.** Long-running ops accept an
585	   optional `&mut dyn ProgressSink` (or
586	   `mpsc::Sender<Event>`). CLI installs a stderr sink; TUI
587	   a status-bar sink; tests a recording sink. `log` /
588	   `tracing` covers diagnostics but is lossy for structured
589	   progress.
590	5. **No globals, no implicit cwd/env reads in ops.**
591	   Everything an op needs goes in `Workspace` or
592	   `*Options`. CLI resolves cwd/env once at startup and
593	   builds the handle. This is what lets a multi-window TUI
594	   drive multiple workspaces without cross-contamination.
595	
596	### Completion stays at the clap layer
597	
598	- Static structure (`-h`, subcommand layout, flag presence):
599	  clap derive on `InitArgs` + leaves. Unchanged from today.
600	- Dynamic value completion (`--account=<TAB>` against
601	  accounts in user config): `ArgValueCompleter` attached to
602	  the leaf in `src/options_flags/`. Already enabled via
603	  `clap_complete`'s `unstable-dynamic` feature. The leaf is
604	  the right home — completion is a clap-aware concern with
605	  no place in `InitOptions`.
606	
607	Ops layer stays clap-free; leaf layer stays clap-aware.
608	Completion drives nothing in the ops layer.
609	
610	### The contained wart
611	
612	`From<&InitArgs> for InitOptions` walks leaf nesting once
613	per subcommand:
614	
615	```rust
616	impl From<&InitArgs> for InitOptions {
617	    fn from(a: &InitArgs) -> Self {
618	        Self {
619	            target: a.target.clone(),
620	            account: a.account.account.clone(),
621	            scope: a.scope.scope,
622	            private: a.provision.private.private,
623	            // ...
624	        }
625	    }
626	}
627	```
628	
629	Verbose but contained: one site per subcommand. Adding a new
630	flag becomes a two-edit change (clap struct + options struct
631	+ one conversion line). Accepted price of decoupling; bodies
632	are flat thereafter.
633	
634	### What this is *not*
635	
636	- **Not a god `Context`.** Two parameters
637	  (`&Workspace`, `&XOptions`), not one merged blob.
638	- **Not premature trait-based DI.** Concrete `Workspace` +
639	  `*Options` structs until a second front-end forces
640	  generalization.
641	- **Not a crate split.** Same crate, separate modules
642	  (e.g. `src/ops/init.rs` vs CLI edge). Promote to a
643	  workspace only when a second consumer crate appears.
644	- **Not removing `src/options_flags/`.** Leaves stay; only
645	  their consumers change shape.
646	
647	### Migration sketch
648	
649	The bot thinks the safest first move is converting one
650	subcommand end-to-end as the worked example before any
651	sweep. `init` is the largest surface; `sync` or `chid` would
652	be a lighter proof of concept.
653	
654	1. Introduce `ops::Workspace` (paths + `UserConfig` only; no
655	   progress sink yet). Wire it into one subcommand.
656	2. Introduce `XOptions` for the chosen first subcommand;
657	   write `From<&XArgs> for XOptions`; port the body to
658	   `&Workspace, &XOptions`.
659	3. Add typed errors + returned outcome for that subcommand.
660	4. Sweep remaining subcommands; each is a contained step.
661	5. (Later) introduce `ProgressSink` when a TUI need actually
662	   surfaces, or a long-running op wants structured progress.
663	
664	The bot thinks the right shape of `Workspace` matters more
665	than the right shape of `InitError` — defer error-type and
666	outcome-type design until step 1 has shaken out.
667	
668	### Open questions
669	
670	- `Workspace` carries the progress sink from day one even
671	  with no consumer? The bot's guess is no — leave it out and
672	  add when a real long-running op forces it; adding later is
673	  mechanical.
674	- `XOptions` owns values (`String`) or borrows (`&str`)? The
675	  bot's guess is owns — simpler, matches the programmatic-
676	  caller path (build then call). Defer borrow optimization
677	  until benchmarks call for it.
678	- Crate split timing — defer until a second consumer crate
679	  appears.
680	
681	## InitParams implementation (0.44.0)
682	
683	Single-step cycle: introduce `Context` + `InitParams` and
684	port `init` to the new shape. Establishes the worked
685	example for the design captured in
686	[Ops layer architecture (forward-looking)](#ops-layer-architecture-forward-looking)
687	above; remaining subcommands defer to later cycles.
688	
689	### Naming differs from "Ops layer architecture" section above
690	
691	Written before this cycle, the design section uses
692	`Workspace` and `XOptions`. This cycle adopts:
693	
694	- `Workspace` → `Context`. Cargo owns "workspace"
695	  formally, and this codebase already uses "workspace"
696	  for the dual-repo project root (`find_workspace_root`).
697	  Doubly overloaded.
698	- `XOptions` → `XParams`. Avoids collision with Rust's
699	  `Option<T>` (`InitParams` fields are a mix of required
700	  and `Option<T>`-typed, which `Options` muddles).
701	  Doesn't collide with `src/options_flags/` either.
702	
703	The forward-looking design section is left as-is for
704	this cycle; once 0.44.0 lands and the worked example
705	is in tree, that section can be retitled / updated
706	during a future close-out so the codebase has one
707	naming.
708	
709	### Why init
710	
711	`args.account.account` ergonomics — the trigger for the
712	whole design — lives here; init also has the deepest
713	leaf nesting (account / repo / scope / private /
714	template / push retry). If the pattern survives init,
715	sync / chid / push / clone port trivially.
716	
717	### In scope
718	
719	- `src/context.rs` (new): `Context` struct holding the
720	  workspace root path + loaded `UserConfig`. Built once
721	  at startup.
722	- `src/init/params.rs` (new): `InitParams` flat plain
723	  struct (owns its values) + `impl From<&InitArgs> for
724	  InitParams`.
725	- `src/init.rs`: port `plan_init` and any other
726	  init-side `pub fn` entrypoints to
727	  `fn (&Context, &InitParams)`. Body reads
728	  `params.account` instead of `args.account.account`.
729	- `src/main.rs`: build `Context` once at startup; the
730	  init dispatch arm passes `(&ctx, &(&args).into())`.
731	- `src/init/tests.rs`: pure-unit tests construct
732	  `Context` + `InitParams` directly (no clap
733	  `try_parse_from` plumbing); integration paths still
734	  exercise the clap edge.
735	
736	### Out of scope (deferred)
737	
738	- Sweep across sync / chid / push / clone / etc.
739	  Each is its own later cycle.
740	- Per chores-09 "five rules": typed errors, returned
741	  outcomes (vs `println!`), `ProgressSink`. Keep this
742	  cycle to the structural split only.
743	- `Context` fields beyond workspace root + user
744	  config. Defer until a real consumer surfaces.
745	
746	### Pre-commit checklist
747	
748	Standard: `cargo fmt`, `cargo clippy --all-targets --
749	-D warnings`, `cargo test`, `cargo install --path .
750	--locked`, retest.
751	
752	### Edits
753	
754	- `Cargo.toml`: bump 0.43.0 → 0.44.0.
755	- `src/context.rs` (new): `Context` struct holding
756	  `user_config: UserConfig` + `Context::load()` ctor;
757	  declared `mod context;` in `src/main.rs`.
758	- `src/init/params.rs` (new): `InitParams` flat struct
759	  + `impl From<&InitArgs> for InitParams` (production
760	  default sets `create_symlink: true`); declared
761	  `mod params; pub use params::InitParams;` in
762	  `src/init.rs`.
763	- `src/init.rs`: `pub fn init` signature changed
764	  from `init(args: &InitArgs, create_symlink: bool)`
765	  to `init(ctx: &Context, params: &InitParams)`
766	  (`create_symlink` folded into `InitParams`).
767	  Internal helpers (`plan_init`, `plan_from_url`,
768	  `plan_from_path`, `plan_from_bare_name`,
769	  `plan_remote`, `plan_local`, `build_plan`,
770	  `create_por`, `create_dual`, `push_repo`,
771	  `run_remote_step`) all switched from
772	  `args: &InitArgs` to `params: &InitParams`. Body
773	  reads `params.account` instead of
774	  `args.account.account` (and similarly across all
775	  leaf nestings). `args.config.resolve(ConfigKind::None)`
776	  call sites collapse to `&params.config` since the
777	  resolve runs at the boundary now. `cfg` inside
778	  `pub fn init` switched from `config::load()?` to
779	  `&ctx.user_config`.
780	- `src/main.rs`: `mod context;` added; init dispatch
781	  builds `Context::load()` then converts
782	  `InitParams::from(&init_args)` and calls
783	  `init::init(&ctx, &params)`.
784	- `src/test_helpers.rs`: `Fixture` and `FixturePor`
785	  builders updated — load `Context`, build
786	  `InitParams::from(&args)`, override
787	  `params.create_symlink = false`, then call
788	  `init(&ctx, &params)`. Imports updated to bring
789	  `Context` and `InitParams` into scope.
790	- `src/init/tests.rs`: every `plan_init(&args, ...)`
791	  call site rewritten to
792	  `plan_init(&InitParams::from(&args), ...)`. The two
793	  `plan_init(&args_for(...), ...)` sites split across
794	  multiple lines for readability. Tests of `InitArgs`
795	  (parse/defaults) untouched — those exercise the
796	  clap edge.
797	- `notes/chores-09.md`: this section + the
798	  `## InitParams implementation (0.44.0)` plan
799	  section.
800	- `notes/todo.md`: drop the cycle's `## In Progress`
801	  entry; add `## Done` entry; rewrite the
802	  `Ops layer / CLI decoupling` `## Todo` item to
803	  reflect "init done, sweep remaining."
804	
```

  "old_string": "## Ops layer architecture (forward-looking)\n\nDesign target for subsequent cycles: separate clap-aware\nparsing from subcommand operation logic so future front-ends\n(e.g. a TUI for bot-conversation exploration, or library\nembedding) call the same core without retrofitting. Captures\nthe conclusion of the 0.42.0-4.7 side discussion about\n`args.account.account` ergonomics — renaming / accessor\nshortcuts were rejected as hiding the architecture mismatch\nrather than fixing it.\n\n### Goals\n\n- Subcommand bodies access flat fields (`opts.account`), not\n  nested clap shapes (`args.account.account`).\n- `src/options_flags/` leaves remain the single source of\n  truth for flag definition (parser, help, completer).\n- Per-subcommand `-h` stays bundle-specific.\n- Tab completion (`vc-x1 init <TAB>`, `--account=<TAB>`)\n  keeps working via clap_complete + dynamic completers.\n- The ops layer is callable without clap as a dependency\n  (today's `plan_init(args: &InitArgs, ...)` is not).\n\n### Two layers, three structures per subcommand\n\n- **CLI layer (clap-aware):** `InitArgs` / `CloneArgs` / etc.\n  `#[derive(Args)]` types that flatten leaves from\n  `src/options_flags/`. Own clap metadata for `-h` and\n  completion. Live at the binary edge.\n- **Ops layer (clap-free):** `InitOptions` / `CloneOptions`\n  — plain structs, flat fields, `Default`. Plus `Workspace`,\n  the shared platform handle (workspace root, loaded\n  `UserConfig`, optional progress sink). Entrypoints are\n  `ops::init(ws: &Workspace, opts: &InitOptions) -> Result<InitOutcome, InitError>`.\n- **Boundary conversion:** `From<&InitArgs> for InitOptions`\n  — one contained site per subcommand where leaf nesting is\n  unpacked. Op bodies never see `args.xxx.xxx`.\n\n### `Workspace` (\"context\") vs per-op options\n\n- **`Workspace`:** shared platform every op runs against —\n  workspace root, loaded user config, optional progress\n  sink. Same shape across subcommands.\n- **`InitOptions`** etc.: per-op input; shape differs by\n  subcommand.\n\nTwo parameters, not a single merged \"god context.\" The\nsignature `fn init(ws: &Workspace, opts: &InitOptions)`\ndocuments what the op depends on; a merged `Context`\ncontaining every possible field would let any op silently\nread any field with no signature-level visibility.\n\nIf `Workspace` itself grows, escape valves (defer until\nneeded):\n\n- Split into `Workspace` (paths + config, read-only, cheap)\n  vs `Session` (progress / cancellation, mutable). Most ops\n  want `&Workspace`; long-running ops also accept\n  `&Session`.\n- Trait-based DI (`fn init<W: HasConfig + HasFs>(...)`) for\n  multiple front-ends with genuinely different platform\n  surfaces. Heavy in Rust; not the default.\n\n### Five rules for the ops layer\n\n1. **Plain options, flat fields, `Default`.** No clap types\n   in `InitOptions`. Domain values like `RepoSelector` are\n   fine (they're domain types); leaf wrappers like\n   `RepoOption` are not.\n2. **Typed errors.** `enum InitError` (likely via\n   `thiserror`), not `Box<dyn Error>`. A TUI matches\n   variants to pick dialogs; CLI formats them.\n   `Box<dyn Error>` discards exactly the information a GUI\n   most needs.\n3. **Returned outcomes, not `println!`.** Each op returns a\n   structured result; CLI formats it for stdout, TUI\n   populates panels. Library writes nothing to stdout\n   itself.\n4. **Progress via a sink.** Long-running ops accept an\n   optional `&mut dyn ProgressSink` (or\n   `mpsc::Sender<Event>`). CLI installs a stderr sink; TUI\n   a status-bar sink; tests a recording sink. `log` /\n   `tracing` covers diagnostics but is lossy for structured\n   progress.\n5. **No globals, no implicit cwd/env reads in ops.**\n   Everything an op needs goes in `Workspace` or\n   `*Options`. CLI resolves cwd/env once at startup and\n   builds the handle. This is what lets a multi-window TUI\n   drive multiple workspaces without cross-contamination.\n\n### Completion stays at the clap layer\n\n- Static structure (`-h`, subcommand layout, flag presence):\n  clap derive on `InitArgs` + leaves. Unchanged from today.\n- Dynamic value completion (`--account=<TAB>` against\n  accounts in user config): `ArgValueCompleter` attached to\n  the leaf in `src/options_flags/`. Already enabled via\n  `clap_complete`'s `unstable-dynamic` feature. The leaf is\n  the right home — completion is a clap-aware concern with\n  no place in `InitOptions`.\n\nOps layer stays clap-free; leaf layer stays clap-aware.\nCompletion drives nothing in the ops layer.\n\n### The contained wart\n\n`From<&InitArgs> for InitOptions` walks leaf nesting once\nper subcommand:\n\n```rust\nimpl From<&InitArgs> for InitOptions {\n    fn from(a: &InitArgs) -> Self {\n        Self {\n            target: a.target.clone(),\n            account: a.account.account.clone(),\n            scope: a.scope.scope,\n            private: a.provision.private.private,\n            // ...\n        }\n    }\n}\n```\n\nVerbose but contained: one site per subcommand. Adding a new\nflag becomes a two-edit change (clap struct + options struct\n+ one conversion line). Accepted price of decoupling; bodies\nare flat thereafter.\n\n### What this is *not*\n\n- **Not a god `Context`.** Two parameters\n  (`&Workspace`, `&XOptions`), not one merged blob.\n- **Not premature trait-based DI.** Concrete `Workspace` +\n  `*Options` structs until a second front-end forces\n  generalization.\n- **Not a crate split.** Same crate, separate modules\n  (e.g. `src/ops/init.rs` vs CLI edge). Promote to a\n  workspace only when a second consumer crate appears.\n- **Not removing `src/options_flags/`.** Leaves stay; only\n  their consumers change shape.\n\n### Migration sketch\n\nThe bot thinks the safest first move is converting one\nsubcommand end-to-end as the worked example before any\nsweep. `init` is the largest surface; `sync` or `chid` would\nbe a lighter proof of concept.\n\n1. Introduce `ops::Workspace` (paths + `UserConfig` only; no\n   progress sink yet). Wire it into one subcommand.\n2. Introduce `XOptions` for the chosen first subcommand;\n   write `From<&XArgs> for XOptions`; port the body to\n   `&Workspace, &XOptions`.\n3. Add typed errors + returned outcome for that subcommand.\n4. Sweep remaining subcommands; each is a contained step.\n5. (Later) introduce `ProgressSink` when a TUI need actually\n   surfaces, or a long-running op wants structured progress.\n\nThe bot thinks the right shape of `Workspace` matters more\nthan the right shape of `InitError` — defer error-type and\noutcome-type design until step 1 has shaken out.\n\n### Open questions\n\n- `Workspace` carries the progress sink from day one even\n  with no consumer? The bot's guess is no — leave it out and\n  add when a real long-running op forces it; adding later is\n  mechanical.\n- `XOptions` owns values (`String`) or borrows (`&str`)? The\n  bot's guess is owns — simpler, matches the programmatic-\n  caller path (build then call). Defer borrow optimization\n  until benchmarks call for it.\n- Crate split timing — defer until a second consumer crate\n  appears.",
  "new_string": "## Subcommand-layer architecture — moved to `ARCHITECTURE.md`\n\nA forward-looking design capture lived here (written\npre-implementation, with the names `Workspace` / `XOptions`).\nIt is now the living [`ARCHITECTURE.md`](../ARCHITECTURE.md)\nat the repo root — the \"Two layers: CLI args vs subcommand\nContext + Params\" section plus Migrations A and B. The\n0.44.0 implementation settled the names as `Context` /\n`XParams`; see `ARCHITECTURE.md` § Naming.\n\nConclusions worth keeping in the journal:\n\n- Two parameters per subcommand (`&Context`, `&XParams`),\n  not one merged \"god context\".\n- Concrete structs, not trait-based DI, until a second\n  front-end forces generalization.\n- Same crate, separate modules — promote to a Cargo\n  workspace only when a second consumer crate appears.\n- `src/options_flags/` leaves stay; only their consumers\n  change shape. Completion stays a clap-layer concern.\n- Convert one subcommand end-to-end as the worked example\n  before any sweep — done in 0.44.0 (`init`); the rest are\n  the \"Subcommand layer / CLI decoupling\" todo item.\n\n`ARCHITECTURE.md` is authoritative; update it (not this\nsection) as the migrations progress."

## Subcommand-layer architecture — moved to `ARCHITECTURE.md`

A forward-looking design capture lived here (written
pre-implementation, with the names `Workspace` / `XOptions`).
It is now the living [`ARCHITECTURE.md`](../ARCHITECTURE.md)
at the repo root — the "Two layers: CLI args vs subcommand
Context + Params" section plus Migrations A and B. The
0.44.0 implementation settled the names as `Context` /
`XParams`; see `ARCHITECTURE.md` § Naming.

Conclusions worth keeping in the journal:

- Two parameters per subcommand (`&Context`, `&XParams`),
  not one merged "god context".
- Concrete structs, not trait-based DI, until a second
  front-end forces generalization.
- Same crate, separate modules — promote to a Cargo
  workspace only when a second consumer crate appears.
- `src/options_flags/` leaves stay; only their consumers
  change shape. Completion stays a clap-layer concern.
- Convert one subcommand end-to-end as the worked example
  before any sweep — done in 0.44.0 (`init`); the rest are
  the "Subcommand layer / CLI decoupling" todo item.

`ARCHITECTURE.md` is authoritative; update it (not this
section) as the migrations progress.

## InitParams implementation (0.44.0)

Single-step cycle: introduce `Context` + `InitParams` and
port `init` to the new shape. Establishes the worked
example for the design now in
[`ARCHITECTURE.md`](../ARCHITECTURE.md); remaining
subcommands defer to later cycles.

### Naming: `Context` / `XParams`

This cycle named the handle `Context` (not `Workspace` —
Cargo owns that word, as does this codebase's
`find_workspace_root`) and the per-subcommand input

```
1	# options_flags — Reusable CLI Options and Flags
2	
3	Per-flag CLI surfaces shared across multiple subcommands. Each
4	shared option or flag (OF) lives in its own submodule so its
5	type, parser, and tests stay together.
6	
7	## Architecture
8	
9	Three composition patterns:
10	
11	- **Leaf** — a `#[derive(Args)]` struct holding the flag(s),
12	  `value_parser`, default, and help text (via the field
13	  doc-comment). One `#[derive(Args)]` per logical OF — single
14	  flag or a small fixed pair (e.g. `push_retry`'s two fields).
15	  Help text aims to be generic enough for any reasonable
16	  consumer.
17	- **Bundle** — a `#[derive(Args)]` that flattens N leaves into a
18	  named role (e.g. `ProvisionOptionFlagBundle`). One
19	  `#[command(flatten)]` line at the consumer picks up the whole
20	  bundle.
21	- **Pattern A escape hatch** — when a consumer needs unique help
22	  text (or different defaults), it skips the leaf's flatten and
23	  inlines its own `#[arg(value_parser = …)]` field, reusing the
24	  leaf's typed value and parser.
25	
26	## Flag vs Option — classify by domain, not wire syntax
27	
28	Every leaf falls into one of two categories, picked by the value
29	domain it carries:
30	
31	- **Flag** — boolean domain. Wire form may be presence/absence
32	  (`--dry-run`), explicit negation (`--no-dry-run`), or value
33	  (`--dry-run=true|false`). All three express a `bool`. Type
34	  name suffix: `*Flag`.
35	- **Option** — non-boolean domain (string, integer, enum, path,
36	  …). Wire form is always `--name=<value>` (or `--name <value>`).
37	  Type name suffix: `*Option` (or `*Options` for a multi-field
38	  leaf like `PushRetryOptions`).
39	
40	Wire form is presentation; the category is the underlying domain.
41	
42	## Adding a new leaf
```

110	impl SquashSpec {
111	    fn parse(s: &str) -> Result<Self, String> {
112	        let parts: Vec<&str> = s.split(',').collect();
113	        if parts.len() != 2 || parts[0].is_empty() || parts[1].is_empty() {
114	            return Err(format!(
115	                "invalid --squash value '{s}': expected SOURCE,TARGET (e.g. @,@-)"
116	            ));
117	        }
118	        Ok(SquashSpec {
119	            source: parts[0].to_string(),
120	            target: parts[1].to_string(),
121	        })
122	    }
123	}

138	    pub fn into_opts(self, log: Option<PathBuf>) -> Result<FinalizeOpts, String> {
139	        let squash = self.squash.map(|s| SquashSpec::parse(&s)).transpose()?;
140	        let push = self.push.is_some();
141	        let bookmark = self.push;

143	        let repo = std::fs::canonicalize(&self.repo)
144	            .map_err(|e| format!("cannot resolve repo path '{}': {e}", self.repo.display()))?;
145	        Ok(FinalizeOpts {
146	            repo,
147	            squash,

149	            bookmark,
150	            push,
151	            log,
152	            detach: self.detach,
153	            exec: self.exec,
154	        })
155	    }
156	}

> AGENT

Good timing to do `finalize` — it's the one that'll shake out the parts `init` didn't exercise.

## What the `finalize` migration actually involves

`finalize` is already ~80% there: `FinalizeArgs` (clap) → `into_opts(cli.log)` → `FinalizeOpts` → `finalize(&opts)`. To bring it to the `init` shape:

- `FinalizeOpts` → **`FinalizeParams`** (naming convention).
- Entry point → **`finalize(ctx: &Context, params: &FinalizeParams)`**.
- Update `ARCHITECTURE.md`'s Migration A table: `finalize` `partial` → `done (0.46.0)`.

Two wrinkles `init` didn't have — I'd want your call on these:

1. **The boundary conversion is fallible.** `into_opts` does `std::fs::canonicalize(repo)` (can fail). `From` can't fail. Options: (a) move the canonicalize *into* `finalize()`'s body so `From<&FinalizeArgs> for FinalizeParams` stays infallible (matches `init`'s shape exactly); (b) use a fallible `TryFrom`-style converter. **My lean: (a)** — keeps the pattern uniform; the op resolving its own paths is fine.

2. **`finalize` needs `cli.log`, which isn't in `FinalizeArgs`.** It comes from the top-level `Cli` (`--log`). Where does it live? (a) Stays a `FinalizeParams` field, passed in at the dispatch arm (`FinalizeParams::from(&args)` then `params.log = cli.log`); (b) **`Context` grows a `log` field**, resolved once at startup like `UserConfig` — `Context::load()` → `Context::from_cli(&cli)` (touches the `init` dispatch too, trivially). **My lean: (b)** — `log` is a startup-resolved platform fact, and this is exactly the "add a `Context` field when a real consumer surfaces" case the architecture anticipates. Makes `finalize` the second worked example in a useful way.

This is **single-step → `0.46.0`** (focused; isolates the `Context`-extension design from the bulk sweep).

## The remaining nine — one step each, one big change, or a multi-step cycle?

**Recommendation: a multi-step cycle (`0.47.0-0` … `0.47.0`), one step per subcommand** — *not* nine separate cycles, *not* one mega-commit.

- *Nine cycles* = too much ceremony (9 version bumps, 9 push+finalize rounds, 9 chores subsections) for what's now a proven, mechanical pattern.
- *One big commit* = ~25–30 files in one diff; fights the per-file review checkpoint rule; bad for bisection if a regression slips through; reviewing it as one unit buys nothing.
- *Multi-step cycle* = one version family, one chores section, but each step is its own bisectable commit, pushed as it lands (per the "flow after every step" rule). `notes/substep-protocol.md` is built for exactly this.

Within that cycle:
- **Clean Migration-A-only steps, one each:** `symlink`, `validate-desc`, `fix-desc`, `clone`, `sync`, then `push` last (biggest surface — resumable state machine, most care). Roughly easiest→hardest.
- **`chid` / `desc` / `list` / `show`:** these four all `#[command(flatten)]` `common::CommonArgs` and are identical-shaped — either do all four in one step (one `CommonArgs`-aware change picks them up), **or** defer them to the cycle that also does the separate "CommonArgs sweep" todo item (Migration B for those four — moving `CommonArgs` into `options_flags/` and dropping `-R` for `--scope`), since for those four Migrations A and B are entangled. I'd lean **defer the four** — don't half-migrate a struct that's about to be restructured anyway.

So: `0.46.0` = finalize. Then `0.47.0-N` = symlink → validate-desc → fix-desc → clone → sync → push (6 steps + plan + close-out). chid/desc/list/show ride along with the CommonArgs sweep later.

Sound right? If so, I'll start the `0.46.0` finalize cycle — version bump first, then the code. And tell me your calls on wrinkles 1 and 2 (I'll go with my leans — (a) and (b) — unless you say otherwise).

> DEVELOPER

1	use std::path::PathBuf;
2	use std::process::Stdio;

10	/// Directory where the detached finalize child writes failure markers.
11	/// A subsequent `vc-x1` invocation scans this directory and surfaces
12	/// any failures to the user, closing the gap that the detached child's
13	/// non-zero exit isn't observable by the caller.
14	fn status_dir() -> Option<PathBuf> {
15	    std::env::var_os("HOME").map(|h| PathBuf::from(h).join(".cache/vc-x1/finalize-status"))
16	}
17	
18	fn write_failure_marker(opts: &FinalizeOpts, err: &str) {
19	    let Some(dir) = status_dir() else {
20	        return;
21	    };
22	    if std::fs::create_dir_all(&dir).is_err() {
23	        return;
24	    }
25	    let ns = SystemTime::now()
26	        .duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH)
27	        .map(|d| d.as_nanos())
28	        .unwrap_or(0); // OK: filesystem-sortable timestamp; 0 on the impossible pre-epoch path
29	    let pid = std::process::id();
30	    let path = dir.join(format!("{ns}-{pid}.status"));
31	    let content = format!(
32	        "timestamp_ns={ns}\npid={pid}\nrepo={}\nbookmark={}\nerror={err}\n",
33	        opts.repo.display(),
34	        opts.bookmark.as_deref().unwrap_or(""),
35	    );
36	    let _ = std::fs::write(&path, content);
37	}
38	
39	/// Read, print, and delete any failure markers left by previous detached
40	/// finalize children. Cheap no-op when the directory doesn't exist.
41	pub fn surface_previous_failures() {
42	    let Some(dir) = status_dir() else {
43	        return;
44	    };
45	    let Ok(entries) = std::fs::read_dir(&dir) else {
46	        return;
47	    };
48	    let mut paths: Vec<PathBuf> = entries
49	        .flatten()
50	        .map(|e| e.path())
51	        .filter(|p| p.extension().and_then(|s| s.to_str()) == Some("status"))
52	        .collect();
53	    paths.sort();
54	    for path in paths {
55	        if let Ok(content) = std::fs::read_to_string(&path) {
56	            eprintln!(
57	                "warn: previous finalize failure ({}):",
58	                path.file_stem()
59	                    .map(|s| s.to_string_lossy())
60	                    .unwrap_or_default() // OK: filename without extension; empty on unexpected absence
61	            );
62	            for line in content.lines() {
63	                eprintln!("  {line}");
64	            }
65	            let _ = std::fs::remove_file(&path);
66	        }
67	    }
68	}

260	
261	/// Return Ok(()) if the revset resolves to one or more commits in `repo`.
262	fn jj_rev_exists(repo: &str, cwd: &std::path::Path, rev: &str) -> Result<(), String> {
263	    run(
264	        "jj",
265	        &["log", "-r", rev, "--no-graph", "-T", "\"x\"", "-R", repo],
266	        cwd,
267	    )
268	    .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
269	    Ok(())
270	}
271	
272	/// Log what finalize is about to do so the user sees the plan before detach.
273	fn log_plan(opts: &FinalizeOpts) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
274	    let repo_str = opts.repo.to_string_lossy();
275	    let cwd = std::path::Path::new(".");
276	
277	    if let Some(ref sq) = opts.squash {
278	        info!(
279	            "finalize: squash {} → {} in {}",
280	            sq.source, sq.target, repo_str
281	        );
282	    }
283	    if let Some(ref bookmark) = opts.bookmark {
284	        let target_rev = opts
285	            .squash
286	            .as_ref()
287	            .map(|sq| sq.target.as_str())
288	            .unwrap_or("@"); // OK: same as above
289	        let current = jj_rev_short(&repo_str, cwd, bookmark).unwrap_or_else(|_| "?".into()); // OK: logging only — fall back to "?" if revset fails
290	        let target = jj_rev_short(&repo_str, cwd, target_rev).unwrap_or_else(|_| "?".into()); // OK: same
291	        info!("finalize: set bookmark '{bookmark}' {current} → {target} ({target_rev})");
292	        if opts.push {
293	            info!("finalize: push '{bookmark}' to remote");
294	        }
295	    }
296	
297	    Ok(())
298	}
299	
300	/// Short one-line summary of a revset: `<change_short> <commit_short>`.
301	fn jj_rev_short(repo: &str, cwd: &std::path::Path, rev: &str) -> Result<String, String> {
302	    run(
303	        "jj",
304	        &[
305	            "log",
306	            "-r",
307	            rev,
308	            "--no-graph",
309	            "-T",
310	            "change_id.shortest(8) ++ \" \" ++ commit_id.shortest(8)",
311	            "-R",
312	            repo,
313	        ],
314	        cwd,
315	    )
316	    .map_err(|e| e.to_string())
317	}
318	
319	fn finalize_exec(opts: &FinalizeOpts) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
320	    debug!("finalize_exec: starting opts={opts:?}");
321	    let repo_str = opts.repo.to_string_lossy();
322	    let cwd = std::path::Path::new(".");
323	
324	    // Squash if requested
325	    if let Some(ref sq) = opts.squash {
326	        let delay = std::time::Duration::from_secs_f64(opts.delay_secs);
327	        debug!("finalize_exec: sleeping {delay:?}");
328	        std::thread::sleep(delay);
329	
330	        info!("finalize: squashing {} → {}...", sq.source, sq.target);
331	        run(
332	            "jj",
333	            &[
334	                "squash",
335	                "--ignore-immutable",
336	                "--use-destination-message",
337	                "--from",
338	                &sq.source,
339	                "--into",
340	                &sq.target,
341	                "-R",
342	                &repo_str,
343	            ],
344	            cwd,
345	        )?;
346	    }
347	
348	    // Set bookmark and push if requested
349	    if let Some(ref bookmark) = opts.bookmark {
350	        // Verify bookmark exists — don't silently create new ones
351	        let result = run("jj", &["bookmark", "list", bookmark, "-R", &repo_str], cwd)?;
352	        if result.is_empty() {
353	            return Err(format!("bookmark '{bookmark}' does not exist").into());
354	        }
355	
356	        let rev = opts
357	            .squash
358	            .as_ref()
359	            .map(|sq| sq.target.as_str())
360	            .unwrap_or("@"); // OK: no squash spec → bookmark points at current @
361	        info!("finalize: setting bookmark '{bookmark}' to {rev}...");
362	        run(
363	            "jj",
364	            &["bookmark", "set", bookmark, "-r", rev, "-R", &repo_str],
365	            cwd,
366	        )?;
367	
368	        if opts.push {
369	            info!("finalize: pushing '{bookmark}' to origin...");
370	            run(
371	                "jj",
372	                &["git", "push", "--bookmark", bookmark, "-R", &repo_str],
373	                &opts.repo,
374	            )?;
375	        }
376	    }
377	
378	    info!("finalize: done");
379	    debug!("finalize_exec: done");
380	    Ok(())
381	}
382	
383	pub fn build_exec_args(opts: &FinalizeOpts) -> Vec<String> {
384	    let mut args = vec![
385	        "finalize".to_string(),
386	        "--exec".to_string(),
387	        "--repo".to_string(),
388	        opts.repo.to_string_lossy().to_string(),
389	    ];
390	    if let Some(ref sq) = opts.squash {
391	        args.push("--squash".to_string());
392	        args.push(format!("{},{}", sq.source, sq.target));
393	        args.push("--delay".to_string());
394	        args.push(opts.delay_secs.to_string());
395	    }
396	    if let Some(ref bookmark) = opts.bookmark {
397	        args.push("--push".to_string());
398	        args.push(bookmark.clone());
399	    }
400	    if let Some(ref log) = opts.log {
401	        args.push("--log".to_string());
402	        args.push(log.to_string_lossy().to_string());
403	    }
404	    args
405	}
406	
407	/// Spawn a detached child process with `--exec` and return immediately.
408	/// The child re-enters `main()`, parses `--exec`, and `finalize()` routes
409	/// it to `finalize_exec()` where the actual work happens.
410	fn detach(opts: &FinalizeOpts) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
411	    debug!("detach: parent starting opts={opts:?}");
412	
413	    let exe = std::env::current_exe()?;
414	    let args = build_exec_args(opts);
415	    debug!("detach: exe={exe:?} args={args:?}");
416	
417	    let mut cmd = std::process::Command::new(exe);
418	    cmd.args(&args).stdin(Stdio::null());
419	
420	    // Hand the child the user's controlling terminal when one exists so
421	    // its output stays visible after the parent exits. Inherited pipes
422	    // are unreliable here — the bash-tool / cron caller typically closes
423	    // its read end on parent exit, silently dropping child writes. Fall
424	    // back to null when there is no tty (pipe-invoked / CI / Windows);
425	    // the log file is authoritative in that case.
426	    match std::fs::OpenOptions::new()
427	        .read(true)
428	        .write(true)
429	        .open("/dev/tty")
430	    {
431	        Ok(tty) => {
432	            debug!("detach: /dev/tty opened, child output → terminal");
433	            let out = tty.try_clone()?;
434	            cmd.stdout(Stdio::from(out));
435	            cmd.stderr(Stdio::from(tty));
436	        }
437	        Err(e) => {
438	            debug!("detach: /dev/tty unavailable ({e}), child output → null");
439	            cmd.stdout(Stdio::null());
440	            cmd.stderr(Stdio::null());
441	        }
442	    }
443	
444	    #[cfg(unix)]
445	    {
446	        use std::os::unix::process::CommandExt;
447	        unsafe {
448	            cmd.pre_exec(|| {
449	                libc::setsid();
450	                Ok(())
451	            });
452	        }
453	    }
454	
455	    let child = cmd.spawn()?;
456	    debug!("detach: spawned child pid={}", child.id());
457	    match &opts.log {
458	        Some(log) => info!(
459	            "finalize: detached (pid {}), log: {}",
460	            child.id(),
461	            log.display()
462	        ),
463	        None => info!("finalize: detached (pid {})", child.id()),
464	    }
465	    Ok(())
466	}
467	
468	pub fn finalize(opts: &FinalizeOpts) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
469	    // Nothing to do — show help hint
470	    if opts.squash.is_none() && !opts.push {
471	        return Err("nothing to do (see --help for options)".into());
472	    }
473	
474	    debug!("finalize: entry opts={opts:?}");
475	
476	    // Validate synchronously before detaching so failures exit with a
477	    // visible non-zero status rather than hiding in the detached child's log.
478	    // Skip in --exec (the re-entered child already passed preflight in the parent).
479	    if !opts.exec {
480	        preflight(opts)?;
481	    }
482	
483	    let result = if opts.detach && !opts.exec {
484	        detach(opts)
485	    } else {
486	        finalize_exec(opts)
487	    };
488	    match &result {
489	        Ok(()) => debug!("finalize: exit ok"),
490	        Err(e) => {
491	            debug!("finalize: exit err={e}");
492	            // In the detached-child path the caller can't observe our
493	            // non-zero exit. Drop a failure marker so a later vc-x1
494	            // invocation can surface the problem.
495	            if opts.exec {
496	                write_failure_marker(opts, &e.to_string());
497	            }
498	        }
499	    }
500	    result
501	}
502	
503	#[cfg(test)]
504	mod tests {
505	    use std::path::PathBuf;
506	
507	    use super::*;
508	    use crate::{Cli, Commands};
509	    use clap::Parser;
510	
511	    fn parse(args: &[&str]) -> FinalizeArgs {
512	        let cli = Cli::try_parse_from(args).unwrap();
513	        match cli.command {
514	            Commands::Finalize(a) => a,
515	            _ => panic!("expected Finalize"),
516	        }
517	    }
518	
519	    fn parse_err(args: &[&str]) -> String {
520	        Cli::try_parse_from(args).unwrap_err().to_string()
521	    }
522	
523	    #[test]
524	    fn no_args() {
525	        let args = parse(&["vc-x1", "finalize"]);
526	        assert_eq!(args.repo, PathBuf::from("."));
527	        assert!(args.squash.is_none());
528	        assert!(args.push.is_none());
529	        assert!(!args.detach);
530	    }
531	
532	    #[test]
533	    fn push_only() {
534	        let args = parse(&["vc-x1", "finalize", "--push", "main"]);
535	        assert!(args.squash.is_none());
536	        assert_eq!(args.push, Some("main".to_string()));
537	    }
538	
539	    #[test]
540	    fn all_opts() {
541	        let cli = Cli::try_parse_from([
542	            "vc-x1",
543	            "--log",
544	            "/tmp/test.log",
545	            "finalize",
546	            "--repo",
547	            ".claude",
548	            "--squash",
549	            "@,@-",
550	            "--push",
551	            "dev-0.14.0",
552	            "--delay",
553	            "2.5",
554	            "--detach",
555	        ])
556	        .unwrap();
557	        assert_eq!(cli.log, Some(PathBuf::from("/tmp/test.log")));
558	        if let Commands::Finalize(args) = cli.command {
559	            assert_eq!(args.repo, PathBuf::from(".claude"));
560	            assert_eq!(args.squash, Some("@,@-".to_string()));
561	            assert_eq!(args.push, Some("dev-0.14.0".to_string()));
562	            assert_eq!(args.delay, 2.5);
563	            assert!(args.detach);
564	        } else {
565	            panic!("expected Finalize");
566	        }
567	    }
568	
569	    #[test]
570	    fn bare_squash() {
571	        let args = parse(&["vc-x1", "finalize", "--squash", "--push", "main"]);
572	        assert_eq!(args.squash, Some("@,@-".to_string()));
573	        assert_eq!(args.push, Some("main".to_string()));
574	    }
575	
576	    #[test]
577	    fn squash_parse_valid() {
578	        let sq = SquashSpec::parse("@,@-").unwrap();
579	        assert_eq!(sq.source, "@");
580	        assert_eq!(sq.target, "@-");
581	    }
582	
583	    #[test]
584	    fn squash_parse_invalid() {
585	        assert!(SquashSpec::parse("@").is_err());
586	        assert!(SquashSpec::parse(",").is_err());
587	        assert!(SquashSpec::parse("@,").is_err());
588	        assert!(SquashSpec::parse(",@-").is_err());
589	    }
590	
591	    #[test]
592	    fn bad_delay() {
593	        let err = parse_err(&["vc-x1", "finalize", "--delay", "abc"]);
594	        assert!(err.contains("invalid value"));
595	    }
596	
597	    #[test]
598	    fn unknown_opt() {
599	        let err = parse_err(&["vc-x1", "finalize", "--bogus"]);
600	        assert!(err.contains("--bogus"));
601	    }
602	
603	    #[test]
604	    fn build_exec_args_roundtrip() {
605	        let opts = FinalizeOpts {
606	            repo: PathBuf::from(".claude"),
607	            squash: Some(SquashSpec {
608	                source: "@".to_string(),
609	                target: "@-".to_string(),
610	            }),
611	            delay_secs: 2.0,
612	            bookmark: Some("dev-0.14.0".to_string()),
613	            push: true,
614	            log: Some(PathBuf::from("/tmp/test.log")),
615	            detach: false,
616	            exec: false,
617	        };
618	        let exec_args = build_exec_args(&opts);
619	        let mut full_args = vec!["vc-x1".to_string()];
620	        full_args.extend(exec_args);
621	        let cli = Cli::try_parse_from(full_args).unwrap();
622	        if let crate::Commands::Finalize(args) = cli.command {
623	            let parsed = args
624	                .into_opts(Some(PathBuf::from("/tmp/test.log")))
625	                .unwrap();
626	            // repo is canonicalized, so compare the canonical form
627	            assert_eq!(parsed.repo, std::fs::canonicalize(&opts.repo).unwrap());
628	            assert_eq!(parsed.squash.as_ref().unwrap().source, "@");
629	            assert_eq!(parsed.squash.as_ref().unwrap().target, "@-");
630	            assert_eq!(parsed.bookmark, Some("dev-0.14.0".to_string()));
631	            assert_eq!(parsed.delay_secs, opts.delay_secs);
632	            assert!(parsed.push);
633	            assert_eq!(parsed.log, opts.log);
634	            assert!(parsed.exec);
635	        } else {
636	            panic!("expected Finalize");
637	        }
638	    }
639	}

  "content": "//! Shared platform handle passed to every subcommand operation.\n//!\n//! `Context` holds platform state that is the same across every\n//! subcommand: today, the loaded `UserConfig` and the resolved\n//! `--log` path. Built once at CLI startup and threaded through\n//! to the subcommand layer.\n//!\n//! The shape is deliberately minimal — fields like project root,\n//! progress sinks, etc. are added when a real consumer surfaces.\n//! See `ARCHITECTURE.md` for the CLI-args vs subcommand\n//! Context+Params layering rationale.\n\nuse std::path::PathBuf;\n\nuse crate::config::{self, UserConfig};\n\n/// Shared platform handle for subcommand operations.\n///\n/// - `user_config`: the loaded user config\n///   (`~/.config/vc-x1/config.toml` or its discovered equivalent).\n/// - `log`: the `--log` path, if given — where file-bound output\n///   goes (and what a re-exec'd child is told to log to).\npub struct Context {\n    pub user_config: UserConfig,\n    pub log: Option<PathBuf>,\n}\n\nimpl Context {\n    /// Build a `Context` by loading the user config from disk and\n    /// taking the CLI-resolved `--log` path.\n    pub fn load(log: Option<PathBuf>) -> Result<Self, Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {\n        Ok(Self {\n            user_config: config::load()?,\n            log,\n        })\n    }\n}\n"
}
