{
  "file_path": "/home/wink/data/prgs/rust/vc-x1/src/finalize.rs",
  "content": "//! The `finalize` subcommand: squash + set-bookmark + push a jj\n//! repo, optionally detached so trailing writes land first.\n//!\n//! Built for the bot to atomically finalize its `.claude` session\n//! repo at end-of-step. Every action is opt-in (`--squash`,\n//! `--push`, `--detach`, `--delay`). The detached path re-execs\n//! `vc-x1 finalize --exec …` so its output and errors survive the\n//! parent exiting; failures are dropped as markers under\n//! `~/.cache/vc-x1/finalize-status` and surfaced on the next\n//! `vc-x1` run.\n\nuse std::path::{Path, PathBuf};\nuse std::process::Stdio;\nuse std::time::{SystemTime, UNIX_EPOCH};\n\nuse clap::Args;\nuse log::{debug, info};\n\nuse crate::common::run;\nuse crate::context::Context;\n\n/// Directory where the detached finalize child writes failure markers.\n/// A subsequent `vc-x1` invocation scans this directory and surfaces\n/// any failures to the user, closing the gap that the detached child's\n/// non-zero exit isn't observable by the caller.\nfn status_dir() -> Option<PathBuf> {\n    std::env::var_os(\"HOME\").map(|h| PathBuf::from(h).join(\".cache/vc-x1/finalize-status\"))\n}\n\n/// Drop a failure marker for the detached child whose non-zero exit\n/// the caller can't observe.\nfn write_failure_marker(params: &FinalizeParams, err: &str) {\n    let Some(dir) = status_dir() else {\n        return;\n    };\n    if std::fs::create_dir_all(&dir).is_err() {\n        return;\n    }\n    let ns = SystemTime::now()\n        .duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH)\n        .map(|d| d.as_nanos())\n        .unwrap_or(0); // OK: filesystem-sortable timestamp; 0 on the impossible pre-epoch path\n    let pid = std::process::id();\n    let path = dir.join(format!(\"{ns}-{pid}.status\"));\n    let content = format!(\n        \"timestamp_ns={ns}\\npid={pid}\\nrepo={}\\nbookmark={}\\nerror={err}\\n\",\n        params.repo.display(),\n        params.bookmark.as_deref().unwrap_or(\"\"),\n    );\n    let _ = std::fs::write(&path, content);\n}\n\n/// Read, print, and delete any failure markers left by previous detached\n/// finalize children. Cheap no-op when the directory doesn't exist.\npub fn surface_previous_failures() {\n    let Some(dir) = status_dir() else {\n        return;\n    };\n    let Ok(entries) = std::fs::read_dir(&dir) else {\n        return;\n    };\n    let mut paths: Vec<PathBuf> = entries\n        .flatten()\n        .map(|e| e.path())\n        .filter(|p| p.extension().and_then(|s| s.to_str()) == Some(\"status\"))\n        .collect();\n    paths.sort();\n    for path in paths {\n        if let Ok(content) = std::fs::read_to_string(&path) {\n            eprintln!(\n                \"warn: previous finalize failure ({}):\",\n                path.file_stem()\n                    .map(|s| s.to_string_lossy())\n                    .unwrap_or_default() // OK: filename without extension; empty on unexpected absence\n            );\n            for line in content.lines() {\n                eprintln!(\"  {line}\");\n            }\n            let _ = std::fs::remove_file(&path);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n/// Squash, set bookmark, and/or push a jj repo.\n///\n/// Designed for the bot to atomically finalize its session repo:\n/// --detach exits immediately, --delay waits for trailing writes,\n/// --squash folds them in, --bookmark + --push sends it upstream.\n/// Every flag is opt-in. See README.md for details.\n#[derive(Args, Debug)]\npub struct FinalizeArgs {\n    /// Path to jj repo\n    #[arg(long, default_value = \".\")]\n    pub repo: PathBuf,\n\n    /// Squash SOURCE into TARGET [default: @,@-]\n    #[arg(long, value_name = \"SOURCE,TARGET\", default_missing_value = \"@,@-\", num_args = 0..=1)]\n    pub squash: Option<String>,\n\n    /// Seconds to wait before squashing\n    #[arg(long, default_value_t = 10.0)]\n    pub delay: f64,\n\n    /// Existing bookmark to push to remote\n    #[arg(long, value_name = \"BOOKMARK\")]\n    pub push: Option<String>,\n\n    /// Detach and run in the background\n    #[arg(long)]\n    pub detach: bool,\n\n    /// Internal: run exec path (used by detach)\n    #[arg(long, hide = true)]\n    pub exec: bool,\n}\n\n/// Parsed squash spec: source and target revisions.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone)]\npub struct SquashSpec {\n    pub source: String,\n    pub target: String,\n}\n\nimpl SquashSpec {\n    /// Parse a `SOURCE,TARGET` pair (e.g. `@,@-`); both halves\n    /// must be non-empty.\n    fn parse(s: &str) -> Result<Self, String> {\n        let parts: Vec<&str> = s.split(',').collect();\n        if parts.len() != 2 || parts[0].is_empty() || parts[1].is_empty() {\n            return Err(format!(\n                \"invalid --squash value '{s}': expected SOURCE,TARGET (e.g. @,@-)\"\n            ));\n        }\n        Ok(SquashSpec {\n            source: parts[0].to_string(),\n            target: parts[1].to_string(),\n        })\n    }\n}\n\n/// Per-invocation finalize inputs — the clap-free shape the\n/// `finalize` op works against. Built from `FinalizeArgs` at the\n/// binary edge via `TryFrom` (fallible: `--squash` parsing and\n/// repo-path canonicalization). The `--log` path lives on\n/// `Context`, not here.\n#[derive(Debug)]\npub struct FinalizeParams {\n    pub repo: PathBuf,\n    pub squash: Option<SquashSpec>,\n    pub delay_secs: f64,\n    pub bookmark: Option<String>,\n    pub push: bool,\n    pub detach: bool,\n    pub exec: bool,\n}\n\nimpl TryFrom<&FinalizeArgs> for FinalizeParams {\n    type Error = String;\n\n    /// Parse `--squash`, derive `push`/`bookmark` from `--push`,\n    /// and canonicalize `--repo` (so the detached child resolves it\n    /// regardless of cwd).\n    fn try_from(a: &FinalizeArgs) -> Result<Self, String> {\n        let squash = a.squash.as_deref().map(SquashSpec::parse).transpose()?;\n        let repo = std::fs::canonicalize(&a.repo)\n            .map_err(|e| format!(\"cannot resolve repo path '{}': {e}\", a.repo.display()))?;\n        Ok(FinalizeParams {\n            repo,\n            squash,\n            delay_secs: a.delay,\n            bookmark: a.push.clone(),\n            push: a.push.is_some(),\n            detach: a.detach,\n            exec: a.exec,\n        })\n    }\n}\n\n/// Validate inputs synchronously before detaching.\n///\n/// Catches the common failure modes up front so the parent can exit\n/// with a visible non-zero status — rather than discovering them in\n/// the detached child where errors only reach the log file.\nfn preflight(params: &FinalizeParams) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {\n    debug!(\"preflight: checking params\");\n    let repo_str = params.repo.to_string_lossy();\n    let cwd = std::path::Path::new(\".\");\n\n    // Verify squash revsets resolve to something.\n    if let Some(ref sq) = params.squash {\n        jj_rev_exists(&repo_str, cwd, &sq.source)\n            .map_err(|e| format!(\"squash source '{}' does not resolve: {e}\", sq.source))?;\n        jj_rev_exists(&repo_str, cwd, &sq.target)\n            .map_err(|e| format!(\"squash target '{}' does not resolve: {e}\", sq.target))?;\n    }\n\n    // Refuse to operate on a repo with conflicts.\n    let conflicts = run(\n        \"jj\",\n        &[\n            \"log\",\n            \"-r\",\n            \"conflicts()\",\n            \"--no-graph\",\n            \"-T\",\n            \"\\\"x\\\"\",\n            \"-R\",\n            &repo_str,\n        ],\n        cwd,\n    )?;\n    if !conflicts.is_empty() {\n        return Err(format!(\"repo '{repo_str}' has conflicts — resolve before finalize\").into());\n    }\n\n    // Bookmark: existence, tracking, forward-only move, push-target description.\n    if let Some(ref bookmark) = params.bookmark {\n        let exists = run(\"jj\", &[\"bookmark\", \"list\", bookmark, \"-R\", &repo_str], cwd)?;\n        if exists.is_empty() {\n            return Err(format!(\"bookmark '{bookmark}' does not exist\").into());\n        }\n\n        crate::common::verify_tracking(&params.repo, bookmark)?;\n\n        let target_rev = params\n            .squash\n            .as_ref()\n            .map(|sq| sq.target.as_str())\n            .unwrap_or(\"@\"); // OK: no squash spec → bookmark will point at current @\n        let range = run(\n            \"jj\",\n            &[\n                \"log\",\n                \"-r\",\n                &format!(\"{bookmark}::({target_rev})\"),\n                \"--no-graph\",\n                \"-T\",\n                \"\\\"x\\\"\",\n                \"-R\",\n                &repo_str,\n            ],\n            cwd,\n        )?;\n        if range.is_empty() {\n            return Err(format!(\n                \"bookmark '{bookmark}' move is not forward — current position is not an \\\n                 ancestor of '{target_rev}' (would diverge)\"\n            )\n            .into());\n        }\n\n        if params.push {\n            let desc = run(\n                \"jj\",\n                &[\n                    \"log\",\n                    \"-r\",\n                    target_rev,\n                    \"--no-graph\",\n                    \"-T\",\n                    \"description\",\n                    \"-R\",\n                    &repo_str,\n                ],\n                cwd,\n            )?;\n            if desc.trim().is_empty() {\n                return Err(format!(\n                    \"push target '{target_rev}' has no description — push would fail \\\n                     (run `jj describe -r {target_rev} -R {repo_str}` first)\"\n                )\n                .into());\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    log_plan(params)?;\n\n    Ok(())\n}\n\n/// Return Ok(()) if the revset resolves to one or more commits in `repo`.\nfn jj_rev_exists(repo: &str, cwd: &std::path::Path, rev: &str) -> Result<(), String> {\n    run(\n        \"jj\",\n        &[\"log\", \"-r\", rev, \"--no-graph\", \"-T\", \"\\\"x\\\"\", \"-R\", repo],\n        cwd,\n    )\n    .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;\n    Ok(())\n}\n\n/// Log what finalize is about to do so the user sees the plan before detach.\nfn log_plan(params: &FinalizeParams) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {\n    let repo_str = params.repo.to_string_lossy();\n    let cwd = std::path::Path::new(\".\");\n\n    if let Some(ref sq) = params.squash {\n        info!(\n            \"finalize: squash {} → {} in {}\",\n            sq.source, sq.target, repo_str\n        );\n    }\n    if let Some(ref bookmark) = params.bookmark {\n        let target_rev = params\n            .squash\n            .as_ref()\n            .map(|sq| sq.target.as_str())\n            .unwrap_or(\"@\"); // OK: same as above\n        let current = jj_rev_short(&repo_str, cwd, bookmark).unwrap_or_else(|_| \"?\".into()); // OK: logging only — fall back to \"?\" if revset fails\n        let target = jj_rev_short(&repo_str, cwd, target_rev).unwrap_or_else(|_| \"?\".into()); // OK: same\n        info!(\"finalize: set bookmark '{bookmark}' {current} → {target} ({target_rev})\");\n        if params.push {\n            info!(\"finalize: push '{bookmark}' to remote\");\n        }\n    }\n\n    Ok(())\n}\n\n/// Short one-line summary of a revset: `<change_short> <commit_short>`.\nfn jj_rev_short(repo: &str, cwd: &std::path::Path, rev: &str) -> Result<String, String> {\n    run(\n        \"jj\",\n        &[\n            \"log\",\n            \"-r\",\n            rev,\n            \"--no-graph\",\n            \"-T\",\n            \"change_id.shortest(8) ++ \\\" \\\" ++ commit_id.shortest(8)\",\n            \"-R\",\n            repo,\n        ],\n        cwd,\n    )\n    .map_err(|e| e.to_string())\n}\n\n/// Do the squash / set-bookmark / push work — runs in the detached\n/// child when `--detach`, inline otherwise.\nfn finalize_exec(params: &FinalizeParams) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {\n    debug!(\"finalize_exec: starting params={params:?}\");\n    let repo_str = params.repo.to_string_lossy();\n    let cwd = std::path::Path::new(\".\");\n\n    // Squash if requested\n    if let Some(ref sq) = params.squash {\n        let delay = std::time::Duration::from_secs_f64(params.delay_secs);\n        debug!(\"finalize_exec: sleeping {delay:?}\");\n        std::thread::sleep(delay);\n\n        info!(\"finalize: squashing {} → {}...\", sq.source, sq.target);\n        run(\n            \"jj\",\n            &[\n                \"squash\",\n                \"--ignore-immutable\",\n                \"--use-destination-message\",\n                \"--from\",\n                &sq.source,\n                \"--into\",\n                &sq.target,\n                \"-R\",\n                &repo_str,\n            ],\n            cwd,\n        )?;\n    }\n\n    // Set bookmark and push if requested\n    if let Some(ref bookmark) = params.bookmark {\n        // Verify bookmark exists — don't silently create new ones\n        let result = run(\"jj\", &[\"bookmark\", \"list\", bookmark, \"-R\", &repo_str], cwd)?;\n        if result.is_empty() {\n            return Err(format!(\"bookmark '{bookmark}' does not exist\").into());\n        }\n\n        let rev = params\n            .squash\n            .as_ref()\n            .map(|sq| sq.target.as_str())\n            .unwrap_or(\"@\"); // OK: no squash spec → bookmark points at current @\n        info!(\"finalize: setting bookmark '{bookmark}' to {rev}...\");\n        run(\n            \"jj\",\n            &[\"bookmark\", \"set\", bookmark, \"-r\", rev, \"-R\", &repo_str],\n            cwd,\n        )?;\n\n        if params.push {\n            info!(\"finalize: pushing '{bookmark}' to origin...\");\n            run(\n                \"jj\",\n                &[\"git\", \"push\", \"--bookmark\", bookmark, \"-R\", &repo_str],\n                &params.repo,\n            )?;\n        }\n    }\n\n    info!(\"finalize: done\");\n    debug!(\"finalize_exec: done\");\n    Ok(())\n}\n\n/// Build the argv for the detached re-exec: `finalize --exec …`,\n/// mirroring `params` plus the `--log` path so the child logs to\n/// the same file.\npub fn build_exec_args(params: &FinalizeParams, log: Option<&Path>) -> Vec<String> {\n    let mut args = vec![\n        \"finalize\".to_string(),\n        \"--exec\".to_string(),\n        \"--repo\".to_string(),\n        params.repo.to_string_lossy().to_string(),\n    ];\n    if let Some(ref sq) = params.squash {\n        args.push(\"--squash\".to_string());\n        args.push(format!(\"{},{}\", sq.source, sq.target));\n        args.push(\"--delay\".to_string());\n        args.push(params.delay_secs.to_string());\n    }\n    if let Some(ref bookmark) = params.bookmark {\n        args.push(\"--push\".to_string());\n        args.push(bookmark.clone());\n    }\n    if let Some(log) = log {\n        args.push(\"--log\".to_string());\n        args.push(log.to_string_lossy().to_string());\n    }\n    args\n}\n\n/// Spawn a detached child process with `--exec` and return immediately.\n/// The child re-enters `main()`, parses `--exec`, and `finalize()` routes\n/// it to `finalize_exec()` where the actual work happens.\nfn detach(ctx: &Context, params: &FinalizeParams) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {\n    debug!(\"detach: parent starting params={params:?}\");\n\n    let exe = std::env::current_exe()?;\n    let args = build_exec_args(params, ctx.log.as_deref());\n    debug!(\"detach: exe={exe:?} args={args:?}\");\n\n    let mut cmd = std::process::Command::new(exe);\n    cmd.args(&args).stdin(Stdio::null());\n\n    // Hand the child the user's controlling terminal when one exists so\n    // its output stays visible after the parent exits. Inherited pipes\n    // are unreliable here — the bash-tool / cron caller typically closes\n    // its read end on parent exit, silently dropping child writes. Fall\n    // back to null when there is no tty (pipe-invoked / CI / Windows);\n    // the log file is authoritative in that case.\n    match std::fs::OpenOptions::new()\n        .read(true)\n        .write(true)\n        .open(\"/dev/tty\")\n    {\n        Ok(tty) => {\n            debug!(\"detach: /dev/tty opened, child output → terminal\");\n            let out = tty.try_clone()?;\n            cmd.stdout(Stdio::from(out));\n            cmd.stderr(Stdio::from(tty));\n        }\n        Err(e) => {\n            debug!(\"detach: /dev/tty unavailable ({e}), child output → null\");\n            cmd.stdout(Stdio::null());\n            cmd.stderr(Stdio::null());\n        }\n    }\n\n    #[cfg(unix)]\n    {\n        use std::os::unix::process::CommandExt;\n        unsafe {\n            cmd.pre_exec(|| {\n                libc::setsid();\n                Ok(())\n            });\n        }\n    }\n\n    let child = cmd.spawn()?;\n    debug!(\"detach: spawned child pid={}\", child.id());\n    match &ctx.log {\n        Some(log) => info!(\n            \"finalize: detached (pid {}), log: {}\",\n            child.id(),\n            log.display()\n        ),\n        None => info!(\"finalize: detached (pid {})\", child.id()),\n    }\n    Ok(())\n}\n\n/// Run the `finalize` subcommand: preflight, then either detach a\n/// child to do the work or do it inline.\npub fn finalize(ctx: &Context, params: &FinalizeParams) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {\n    // Nothing to do — show help hint\n    if params.squash.is_none() && !params.push {\n        return Err(\"nothing to do (see --help for options)\".into());\n    }\n\n    debug!(\"finalize: entry params={params:?}\");\n\n    // Validate synchronously before detaching so failures exit with a\n    // visible non-zero status rather than hiding in the detached child's log.\n    // Skip in --exec (the re-entered child already passed preflight in the parent).\n    if !params.exec {\n        preflight(params)?;\n    }\n\n    let result = if params.detach && !params.exec {\n        detach(ctx, params)\n    } else {\n        finalize_exec(params)\n    };\n    match &result {\n        Ok(()) => debug!(\"finalize: exit ok\"),\n        Err(e) => {\n            debug!(\"finalize: exit err={e}\");\n            // In the detached-child path the caller can't observe our\n            // non-zero exit. Drop a failure marker so a later vc-x1\n            // invocation can surface the problem.\n            if params.exec {\n                write_failure_marker(params, &e.to_string());\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    result\n}\n\n#[cfg(test)]\nmod tests {\n    use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};\n\n    use super::*;\n    use crate::{Cli, Commands};\n    use clap::Parser;\n\n    fn parse(args: &[&str]) -> FinalizeArgs {\n        let cli = Cli::try_parse_from(args).unwrap();\n        match cli.command {\n            Commands::Finalize(a) => a,\n            _ => panic!(\"expected Finalize\"),\n        }\n    }\n\n    fn parse_err(args: &[&str]) -> String {\n        Cli::try_parse_from(args).unwrap_err().to_string()\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn no_args() {\n        let args = parse(&[\"vc-x1\", \"finalize\"]);\n        assert_eq!(args.repo, PathBuf::from(\".\"));\n        assert!(args.squash.is_none());\n        assert!(args.push.is_none());\n        assert!(!args.detach);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn push_only() {\n        let args = parse(&[\"vc-x1\", \"finalize\", \"--push\", \"main\"]);\n        assert!(args.squash.is_none());\n        assert_eq!(args.push, Some(\"main\".to_string()));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn all_opts() {\n        let cli = Cli::try_parse_from([\n            \"vc-x1\",\n            \"--log\",\n            \"/tmp/test.log\",\n            \"finalize\",\n            \"--repo\",\n            \".claude\",\n            \"--squash\",\n            \"@,@-\",\n            \"--push\",\n            \"dev-0.14.0\",\n            \"--delay\",\n            \"2.5\",\n            \"--detach\",\n        ])\n        .unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(cli.log, Some(PathBuf::from(\"/tmp/test.log\")));\n        if let Commands::Finalize(args) = cli.command {\n            assert_eq!(args.repo, PathBuf::from(\".claude\"));\n            assert_eq!(args.squash, Some(\"@,@-\".to_string()));\n            assert_eq!(args.push, Some(\"dev-0.14.0\".to_string()));\n            assert_eq!(args.delay, 2.5);\n            assert!(args.detach);\n        } else {\n            panic!(\"expected Finalize\");\n        }\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn bare_squash() {\n        let args = parse(&[\"vc-x1\", \"finalize\", \"--squash\", \"--push\", \"main\"]);\n        assert_eq!(args.squash, Some(\"@,@-\".to_string()));\n        assert_eq!(args.push, Some(\"main\".to_string()));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn squash_parse_valid() {\n        let sq = SquashSpec::parse(\"@,@-\").unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(sq.source, \"@\");\n        assert_eq!(sq.target, \"@-\");\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn squash_parse_invalid() {\n        assert!(SquashSpec::parse(\"@\").is_err());\n        assert!(SquashSpec::parse(\",\").is_err());\n        assert!(SquashSpec::parse(\"@,\").is_err());\n        assert!(SquashSpec::parse(\",@-\").is_err());\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn bad_delay() {\n        let err = parse_err(&[\"vc-x1\", \"finalize\", \"--delay\", \"abc\"]);\n        assert!(err.contains(\"invalid value\"));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn unknown_opt() {\n        let err = parse_err(&[\"vc-x1\", \"finalize\", \"--bogus\"]);\n        assert!(err.contains(\"--bogus\"));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn try_from_canonicalizes_repo() {\n        let args = parse(&[\"vc-x1\", \"finalize\", \"--repo\", \".\", \"--push\", \"main\"]);\n        let params = FinalizeParams::try_from(&args).unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(params.repo, std::fs::canonicalize(\".\").unwrap());\n        assert_eq!(params.bookmark, Some(\"main\".to_string()));\n        assert!(params.push);\n        assert!(params.squash.is_none());\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn try_from_bad_squash() {\n        let args = parse(&[\"vc-x1\", \"finalize\", \"--squash\", \"@\", \"--push\", \"main\"]);\n        assert!(FinalizeParams::try_from(&args).is_err());\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn build_exec_args_roundtrip() {\n        let params = FinalizeParams {\n            repo: PathBuf::from(\".claude\"),\n            squash: Some(SquashSpec {\n                source: \"@\".to_string(),\n                target: \"@-\".to_string(),\n            }),\n            delay_secs: 2.0,\n            bookmark: Some(\"dev-0.14.0\".to_string()),\n            push: true,\n            detach: false,\n            exec: false,\n        };\n        let exec_args = build_exec_args(&params, Some(Path::new(\"/tmp/test.log\")));\n        let mut full_args = vec![\"vc-x1\".to_string()];\n        full_args.extend(exec_args);\n        let cli = Cli::try_parse_from(full_args).unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(cli.log, Some(PathBuf::from(\"/tmp/test.log\")));\n        if let crate::Commands::Finalize(args) = cli.command {\n            let parsed = FinalizeParams::try_from(&args).unwrap();\n            // repo is canonicalized, so compare the canonical form\n            assert_eq!(parsed.repo, std::fs::canonicalize(&params.repo).unwrap());\n            assert_eq!(parsed.squash.as_ref().unwrap().source, \"@\");\n            assert_eq!(parsed.squash.as_ref().unwrap().target, \"@-\");\n            assert_eq!(parsed.bookmark, Some(\"dev-0.14.0\".to_string()));\n            assert_eq!(parsed.delay_secs, params.delay_secs);\n            assert!(parsed.push);\n            assert!(parsed.exec);\n        } else {\n            panic!(\"expected Finalize\");\n        }\n    }\n}\n"

Code unit done — `0.46.0` finalize migration, all checks green.

**Changes:**
- `Cargo.toml`: 0.45.0 → 0.46.0.
- `src/context.rs`: `Context` gains `pub log: Option<PathBuf>`; `Context::load()` → `Context::load(log: Option<PathBuf>)`; doc updated.
- `src/main.rs`: init arm passes `cli.log` to `Context::load`; finalize arm builds `Context::load(cli.log)` + `FinalizeParams::try_from(&finalize_args)` + `finalize::finalize(&ctx, &params)` (was `finalize_args.into_opts(cli.log)` + `finalize(&opts)`).
- `src/finalize.rs`: added module `//!` docstring (it had none); `FinalizeOpts` → `FinalizeParams` (dropped its `log` field — now on `Context`); `FinalizeArgs::into_opts(self, log)` → `impl TryFrom<&FinalizeArgs> for FinalizeParams`; `finalize`/`detach` now `(ctx: &Context, params: &FinalizeParams)`, read `ctx.log`; `build_exec_args(params, log: Option<&Path>)`; `preflight`/`finalize_exec`/`log_plan`/`write_failure_marker` param renamed `opts`→`params`; tests updated, +2 new (`try_from_canonicalizes_repo`, `try_from_bad_squash`).
- `src/test_helpers.rs`: `Context::load()` → `Context::load(None)` (×2).

**Decisions on the two wrinkles:**
- *Boundary conversion* → **`TryFrom`** (fallible), not infallible `From`. Making it infallible would mean keeping `squash` as a raw `Option<String>` and re-parsing it at ~4 use sites (or scattering the `canonicalize` into the op body + threading the canonical path through `build_exec_args`). That's the "fragile" path you flagged — `TryFrom` keeps parse + canonicalize at the edge, returns real errors, zero unwraps. (Small refinement to `ARCHITECTURE.md` falls out: boundary is `From<&XArgs>` when infallible, `TryFrom` when it does fallible normalization.)
- *`--log`* → **`Context.log`**. `finalize` genuinely needs it (the detached re-exec's `--log` arg), so it's a real first consumer of a non-`UserConfig` `Context` field — validates the "grow `Context` when a consumer surfaces" path.

**Checks:** `cargo fmt` clean · `cargo clippy --all-targets -- -D warnings` clean · `cargo test` 360 + 5 + 4 + 5 all pass · `cargo install --path . --locked` → `vc-x1 0.46.0` · retest clean.

Still to do for this cycle (docs — on your go): `ARCHITECTURE.md` (Migration A table `finalize` → done; `Context` bullet mentions `log`; `From`/`TryFrom` note), `notes/chores-09.md` (0.46.0 entry), `notes/todo.md` (item #1 update + Done entry + `[86]`). Then `vc-x1 push main`.

> AGENT

Three good threads — let me take them in order.

## 1. `From` (init) vs `TryFrom` (finalize) — is the asymmetry wrong?

It's actually the *principled* split, not an accident: `From` is for total conversions, `TryFrom` for fallible ones — that's literally what the two traits are for.
- `InitParams::from(&InitArgs)` is just moving/cloning already-validated leaf values — it genuinely cannot fail.
- `FinalizeParams::try_from(&FinalizeArgs)` parses the composite `--squash` value and canonicalizes `--repo` — it genuinely can.

Forcing `TryFrom` everywhere would mean `impl TryFrom<&InitArgs> for InitParams { type Error = Infallible; … }` — an `Error` type that can't be constructed. That's the recognized anti-pattern, not the cure.

And here's the thing that defuses most of the "inconsistency" worry: **std already gives you `TryFrom` for free wherever you `impl From`** (blanket `impl<T, U: Into<T>> TryFrom<U> for T`, `Error = Infallible`). So `InitParams::try_from(&args)` *already compiles* — the dispatch code can be uniform if we want it to be, without touching the trait impls. (Whether `main.rs` actually writes `.try_from()` for the infallible ones — meh, you'd end up `match`ing or `.expect()`ing an unreachable `Err`, which is uglier than just `.from()`. I'd leave init's dispatch arm as `.from()`.)

**My recommendation:** keep the split; codify the rule in `ARCHITECTURE.md` so it reads as a rule, not a quirk — *"boundary conversion is `impl From<&XArgs>` when the conversion is total (moving/cloning leaf values), `impl TryFrom<&XArgs>` when it does fallible normalization — parsing a composite flag value, canonicalizing a path, etc."* That's the one decision that gates finishing this cycle (see bottom).

## 2. `--repo` in init vs finalize — could `options_flags` handle both?

Not as *one* leaf — they're three different flags wearing the same name:
- **init's `--repo`** (`RepoOption`) — *where to create* the new repo: `remote` (URL prefix) / `local` (parent dir for fixture bares).
- **finalize's `--repo`** — *which existing jj repo to operate on* (a `PathBuf`, default `.`).
- **the multi-repo `-R`/`--repo` list** in `CommonArgs` / `sync` — *which repos to query*.

Different domains → different help, defaults, value semantics. They can't collapse. But `options_flags` already has the mechanism for "same spelling, different help/defaults": the **"Pattern A escape hatch"** in its README. So "options_flags handles both" really means "each gets its own leaf there" (e.g. a `TargetRepoOption` for the finalize-style one) — which is exactly point 3.

## 3. Move `--squash` (and the rest of `FinalizeArgs`) into `options_flags`?

Yes — but that's **Migration B for finalize**, which `ARCHITECTURE.md` tracks as a *separate* migration from the Args→Context/Params one we're doing now. Not all of `FinalizeArgs` is equal there:
- **`--squash`** — clear candidate: `vc-x1 push --squash` is already on the todo, so it'll be a genuine shared leaf (`SquashOption`). Worth promoting.
- **`--delay` / `--detach` / `--exec`** — finalize-only today. The `options_flags` discipline is "promote to a leaf when a second consumer appears" — so leave these inline until one does. Pre-emptively leaf-ifying single-use flags adds indirection for no reuse.
- **`--repo` / `--push`** — finalize-flavored; could become leaves if reuse shows up (e.g. a future op that "operates on a jj repo at PATH"), otherwise inline.

I'll add a `notes/todo.md` item for "Migration B: finalize → options_flags (`--squash` shared with push)". **Not** folding it into this 0.46.0 cycle — that cycle is scoped to Migration A, and mixing the two muddies it (same lesson as deferring chid/desc/list/show).

---

So for **right now**: I think we leave 0.46.0 as it stands (Migration A done; `TryFrom` because finalize's boundary is genuinely fallible) and just do the docs — with `ARCHITECTURE.md` now stating the `From`-vs-`TryFrom` rule explicitly, plus the new Migration-B-for-finalize todo.

**One thing I need your call on:** are you OK with the `From`/`TryFrom` split (my rec), or do you want `init` converted to `TryFrom` in this cycle for strict uniformity? If the latter, it's a small add to 0.46.0 — say the word.

30	
31	- **CLI layer (clap-aware)** — `XArgs` `#[derive(Args)]`
32	  structs that flatten leaves from `src/options_flags/`. Own
33	  the clap metadata for `--help` and completion. Live at the
34	  binary edge in each subcommand module.
35	- **Subcommand layer (clap-free)** — `Context` (shared platform:
36	  workspace root, loaded `UserConfig`, later a progress
37	  sink) plus a per-subcommand `XParams` plain struct (flat
38	  fields, owns its values, `Default`). Entrypoint shape:
39	  `fn x(ctx: &Context, params: &XParams) -> Result<…>`.
40	- **Boundary conversion** — `impl From<&XArgs> for XParams`,
41	  one contained site per subcommand, where leaf nesting is
42	  unpacked once. Subcommand bodies read `params.account`,
43	  never `args.account.account`.
44	
45	Two parameters per subcommand; the signature documents what
46	the subcommand depends on. `Context` is the same across all
47	subcommands; `XxxParams` carry the per-subcommand inputs.
48	
49	Rules for the subcommand layer (some still aspirational — see
50	[Migration A](#migration-a--args--context--params)):
51	
52	1. Plain options, flat fields, `Default` — no clap types in
53	   `XParams`. Domain types (`RepoSelector`, `Scope`) are
54	   fine; leaf wrappers (`RepoOption`) are not.
55	2. Typed errors (`enum XError`, not `Box<dyn Error>`) — a
56	   TUI matches variants to pick dialogs; CLI formats them.
57	3. Returned outcomes, not `println!` — each subcommand
58	   returns a structured result; CLI formats it, a library
59	   writes nothing to stdout itself.
60	4. Progress via an optional `&mut dyn ProgressSink` on
61	   long-running subcommands — CLI installs a stderr sink, tests a
62	   recording sink.
63	5. No globals, no implicit cwd/env reads in subcommands — CLI
64	   resolves cwd/env once at startup and builds `Context`.
65	
66	Not a god `Context` (two params, not one blob), not premature
67	trait-based DI (concrete structs until a second front-end
68	forces it), not a crate split (same crate, separate modules —
69	promote to a workspace only when a second consumer crate
70	appears), and not removing `src/options_flags/` (leaves stay;
71	only their consumers change shape).
72	
73	### Naming
74	
75	Earlier drafts of this design (and the `notes/chores-09.md`
76	capture) used `Workspace` / `XOptions`. The implementation
77	(0.44.0) chose `Context` / `XParams` instead:
78	
79	- `Workspace` → `Context` — Cargo owns "workspace" formally,
80	  and this codebase already uses "workspace" for the
81	  dual-repo project root (`find_workspace_root`).
82	- `XOptions` → `XParams` — avoids visual collision with
83	  `Option<T>` (a params struct mixes required and
84	  `Option<T>`-typed fields) and with `src/options_flags/`.
85	
86	## Module map
87	
88	CLI layer:
89	
90	- `src/main.rs` — the clap edge:
91	  - `pub struct Cli` — clap `Parser`.
92	  - `pub enum Commands` — clap `Subcommand`; matched in the
93	    dispatch arms.
94	  - `cli_with_banner()` — walks the command tree adding the
95	    `vc-x1 X.Y.Z` banner as `before_help` on every subcommand.
96	  - `CompleteEnv` wiring for dynamic completion.
97	  - `bm_track` — a permanent `main`-bookmark tracking
98	    diagnostic printed on entry/exit of every command.
99	
100	- `src/options_flags/` — options/flags shared across
101	  subcommands, so a flag is defined once (parser, help,
102	  completer); Migration B tracks what's been moved here so
103	  far. See
104	  [`src/options_flags/README.md`](src/options_flags/README.md).
105	  - reusable **leaves**: `account`, `config`, `dry_run`,
106	    `private`, `push_retry`, `repo`, `scope`, `use_template`.
107	  - reusable **bundles**: `provision_bundle`.
108	- `src/common.rs` — `CommonArgs` (the shared positional-rev /
109	  `-R` repo-list / `-n` / `--limit` / `-L` flag set flattened
110	  by `chid`, `desc`, `list`, `show`) plus the repo-iteration
111	  and revision-resolution helpers they share.
112	
113	Subcommand layer scaffolding:
114	
115	- `src/context.rs` — `Context`: the shared platform handle

119	- `src/scope.rs` — `Scope` enum (`Roles(Vec<Side>)` for the

Entrypoint shape:\n  `fn x(ctx: &Context, params: &XParams) -> Result<…>`.\n- **Boundary conversion** — `impl From<&XArgs> for XParams`,\n  one contained site per subcommand, where leaf nesting is\n  unpacked once.   "new_string": "- **Subcommand layer (clap-free)** — `Context` (shared platform:\n  loaded `UserConfig`, the `--log` path; later a workspace\n  root, a progress sink) plus a per-subcommand `XParams` plain\n  struct (flat fields, owns its values, `Default`). Entrypoint\n  shape: `fn x(ctx: &Context, params: &XParams) -> Result<…>`.\n- **Boundary conversion** — `impl From<&XArgs> for XParams`\n  when the conversion is total (just moving/cloning leaf\n  values); `impl TryFrom<&XArgs> for XParams` when it does\n  fallible normalization — parsing a composite flag value,\n  canonicalizing a path, etc. (e.g. `finalize`). One contained\n  site per subcommand, where leaf nesting is unpacked once.\n  Subcommand bodies read `params.account`, never\n  `args.account.account`."
}

> AGENT

Docs unit done — full `0.46.0` cycle in place.

**`ARCHITECTURE.md`:**
- Migration A table: `finalize` `partial` → `done (0.46.0) — finalize(&Context, &FinalizeParams); TryFrom<&FinalizeArgs> (fallible boundary); --log moved onto Context`; `init` row gains `From<&InitArgs>` for symmetry.
- "Boundary conversion" bullet now states the rule: `From<&XArgs>` when the conversion is total, `TryFrom<&XArgs>` when it does fallible normalization (composite-flag parse, path canonicalize — e.g. `finalize`).
- "Subcommand layer (clap-free)" + `src/context.rs` bullets now mention `Context` carries the `--log` path; `Context::load(log)`.
- subcommand-modules table `finalize.rs` row updated; Migration A intro notes the planned `0.47.0-N` multi-step cycle (one step per subcommand; chid/desc/list/show with the CommonArgs sweep); "out of scope" line updated (`Context` now has `log`).

**`notes/chores-09.md`:** added `## finalize subcommand-layer migration (0.46.0)` — intro + decisions (From/TryFrom split kept; `--log` on `Context`) + `### Edits`.

**`notes/todo.md`:** item #1 rewritten (init + finalize done; remaining nine → `0.47.0-N` plan; chid/desc/list/show ride with CommonArgs sweep); new item *"Migration B: finalize flags → options_flags"* (`--squash`/`SquashSpec` → shared `SquashOption` leaf when `push --squash` lands; `--delay`/`--detach`/`--exec` stay inline until a 2nd consumer; the three `--repo` flavors noted as distinct); `## Done` entry + `[86]` ref.

Diff stat: `src/finalize.rs` (±~190), `ARCHITECTURE.md` (±48), `notes/chores-09.md` (+53), `notes/todo.md` (+29), `src/{context,main,test_helpers}.rs` small, `Cargo.toml`+`Cargo.lock` bump.

**Pre-commit checks:** code unit already ran `cargo fmt` (clean) / `clippy --all-targets -- -D warnings` (clean) / `cargo test` (360+5+4+5 pass) / `cargo install --path . --locked` (→ `vc-x1 0.46.0`) / retest (pass). The docs unit is markdown-only — no build impact; `vc-x1 push` preflight re-runs fmt/clippy/test anyway.

Proposed commit message (same for both repos; push adds the `ochid:` trailer):

**Title**
```
refactor: finalize → Context + FinalizeParams (0.46.0)
```

**Body**
```
Brings `finalize` from "partial" to fully on the subcommand-
layer shape: FinalizeOpts → FinalizeParams via a fallible
TryFrom<&FinalizeArgs> boundary; finalize(&Context,
&FinalizeParams); --log moved onto Context (finalize is its
first non-UserConfig consumer). Kept the From/TryFrom split —
From when the conversion is total (init), TryFrom when it
isn't (finalize). No behavior change.

- Cargo.toml: bump 0.45.0 → 0.46.0.
- src/context.rs: Context gains log: Option<PathBuf>; load() → load(log).
- src/finalize.rs: module docstring; FinalizeOpts → FinalizeParams
  (log moved to Context); into_opts → TryFrom<&FinalizeArgs>;
  finalize/detach take (&Context, &FinalizeParams); build_exec_args(
  params, log); opts→params renames; tests updated, +2.
- src/main.rs: init arm passes cli.log to Context::load; finalize
  arm uses Context + FinalizeParams::try_from.
- src/test_helpers.rs: Context::load() → Context::load(None).
- ARCHITECTURE.md: Migration A table — finalize done (0.46.0);
  boundary-conversion bullet states the From/TryFrom rule; Context
  bullet mentions --log; 0.47.0-N multi-step plan noted.
- notes/todo.md: #1 updated (finalize done; remaining nine →
  0.47.0-N); new "Migration B: finalize flags → options_flags" item;
  Done entry; [86] ref.
- notes/chores-09.md: 0.46.0 subsection.
```

Want changes, or shall I `vc-x1 push main` with this?

177	
178	## Migration A — args → Context + Params
179	
180	Port each subcommand's `pub fn x(args: &XxxArgs)` to
181	`pub fn x(ctx: &Context, params: &XxxParms)`, adding an
182	`XxxParms` flat struct + a `From` (or `TryFrom`, if the
183	conversion is fallible) at the binary edge. `init` (0.44.0)
184	and `finalize` (0.46.0) are done; the rest follow the same
185	shape. The live checklist is the "Subcommand layer / CLI
186	decoupling" item in [`notes/todo.md`](notes/todo.md).
187	
188	| Subcommand | Status |
189	| --- | --- |
190	| `init` | done (0.44.0) — `init(&Context, &InitParams)`; `From<&InitArgs>` |
191	| `finalize` | done (0.46.0) — `finalize(&Context, &FinalizeParams)`; `TryFrom<&FinalizeArgs>` (fallible boundary); `--log` moved onto `Context` |
192	| `sync` | not started |
193	| `chid` | not started |
194	| `desc` | not started |
195	| `list` | not started |
196	| `show` | not started |
197	| `validate-desc` | not started |
198	| `fix-desc` | not started |
199	| `clone` | not started |
200	| `push` | not started |
201	| `symlink` | not started |
202	
203	The remaining nine are planned as one multi-step cycle
204	(`0.47.0-N`), one step per subcommand (`chid`/`desc`/`list`/
205	`show` ride with the separate "CommonArgs sweep" since their
206	Migration A and B are entangled).
207	
208	Out of scope for the early ports (deferred until a real
209	consumer surfaces): typed errors, returned outcomes vs
210	`println!`, the `ProgressSink`, and `Context` fields beyond
211	`UserConfig` + the `--log` path (`finalize` surfaced `log`).
212	
213	## Migration B — per-subcommand flags → `src/options_flags/`
214	
215	Independently of Migration A, per-subcommand inline `#[arg]`
216	fields are being lifted into reusable leaves/bundles under
217	`src/options_flags/` so a flag is defined (parser, help,
218	completer) exactly once. See
219	[`src/options_flags/README.md`](src/options_flags/README.md)
220	for the leaf / bundle / Pattern-A mechanics and the
221	Flag-vs-Option classification.
222	
223	State today:
224	
225	- **`init`** — fully composed from leaves/bundles
226	  (`account`, `repo`, `scope`, the `provision` bundle,
227	  `use_template`, `config`).
228	- **`chid` / `desc` / `list` / `show`** — share
229	  `common::CommonArgs`, a per-domain shared struct rather
230	  than an `options_flags/` leaf. The "CommonArgs sweep"
231	  todo item folds these into the leaf model (and drops the
232	  repeatable `-R`/`--repo` in favor of the `--scope` path
233	  form).
234	- **`sync` / `push` / `clone` / `validate-desc` /
235	  `fix-desc` / `finalize` / `symlink`** — still mostly
236	  inline `#[arg]` fields. The `--scope` retrofits queued in

```
30	
31	- **CLI layer (clap-aware)**:
32	  Primary task is to create XxxParams from user facing
33	  `XxxArgs` `#[derive(Args)]` from clap.
34	  Where there are options/flags used in more than one
35	  subcommand they will reside as flatten leaves in
36	  `src/options_flags/`.
37	- **Subcommand layer (clap-free)**:
38	  Entrypoint shape: `fn x(ctx: &Context, params: &XxxParams) -> Result<…>`.
39	  And this isolates the subcommand from user and clap.
40	  - `Context` — common state shared with all subcommands.
41	  - `XxxParams` — a subcommand's parameters, derived from
42	    config or command-line arguments.
43	- **Boundary conversion**:
44	  Converting `XxxArgs` to `XxxParams` uses either
45	  `impl From<&XxxArgs> for XxxParams` or
46	  `impl TryFrom<&XxxArgs> for XxxParams`, depending on whether
47	  the conversion can fail.
48	
49	Two parameters per subcommand; the signature documents what
50	the subcommand depends on. `Context` is the same across all
51	subcommands; `XxxParams` carry the per-subcommand inputs.
52	
53	Rules for the subcommand layer (some still aspirational — see
54	[Migration A](#migration-a--args--context--params)):
55	
56	1. Plain options, flat fields, `Default` — no clap types in
57	   `XxxParams`. Domain types (`RepoSelector`, `Scope`) are
58	   fine; leaf wrappers (`RepoOption`) are not.
59	2. Typed errors (`enum XError`, not `Box<dyn Error>`) — a
60	   TUI matches variants to pick dialogs; CLI formats them.
61	3. Returned outcomes, not `println!` — each subcommand
62	   returns a structured result; CLI formats it, a library
63	   writes nothing to stdout itself.
64	4. Progress via an optional `&mut dyn ProgressSink` on
65	   long-running subcommands — CLI installs a stderr sink, tests a
66	   recording sink.
67	5. No globals, no implicit cwd/env reads in subcommands — CLI
68	   resolves cwd/env once at startup and builds `Context`.
69	
70	None of this is sacred — the whole structure is an experiment
71	and the repo keeps changing. The current scope is deliberately
72	narrow:
73	
74	- a small `Context` — two params, not one god blob;
75	- no trait-based DI yet;
76	- one crate, not a workspace split;
77	- `src/options_flags/` leaves left in place — only their
78	  consumers change shape.
79	
80	Widen any of it when the evidence (a second front-end, a second
81	consumer crate, a real platform-surface divergence) calls for
82	it — not before.
83	
84	### Naming
85	
86	Earlier drafts of this design (and the `notes/chores-09.md`
87	capture) used `Workspace` / `XOptions`. The implementation
88	(0.44.0) chose `Context` / `XxxParams` instead:
89	
90	- `Workspace` → `Context` — Cargo owns "workspace" formally,
91	  and this codebase already uses "workspace" for the
92	  dual-repo project root (`find_workspace_root`).
93	- `XOptions` → `XxxParams` — avoids visual collision with
94	  `Option<T>` (a params struct mixes required and
95	  `Option<T>`-typed fields) and with `src/options_flags/`.
96	
97	## Module map
```

refactor: finalize → Context + FinalizeParams (0.46.0)

**Body**
```
Brings `finalize` from "partial" to fully on the subcommand-
layer shape: FinalizeOpts → FinalizeParams via a fallible
TryFrom<&FinalizeArgs> boundary; finalize(&Context,
&FinalizeParams); --log moved onto Context (finalize is its
first non-UserConfig consumer). Kept the From/TryFrom split —
From when the conversion is total (init), TryFrom when it
isn't (finalize). No behavior change.

- Cargo.toml: bump 0.45.0 → 0.46.0.
- src/context.rs: Context gains log: Option<PathBuf>; load() → load(log).
- src/finalize.rs: module docstring; FinalizeOpts → FinalizeParams
  (log moved to Context); into_opts → TryFrom<&FinalizeArgs>;
  finalize/detach take (&Context, &FinalizeParams); build_exec_args(
  params, log); opts→params renames; tests updated, +2.
- src/main.rs: init arm passes cli.log to Context::load; finalize
  arm uses Context + FinalizeParams::try_from.
- src/test_helpers.rs: Context::load() → Context::load(None).
- ARCHITECTURE.md: Migration A table — finalize done (0.46.0);
  boundary-conversion states the From (total) / TryFrom (fallible)
  rule; Context bullet mentions --log; "Two layers" bullets
  reworded; scope-boundaries paragraph reframed as an experiment
  caveat (bulleted); typo/grammar fixes (XxxParams, infallible);
  0.47.0-N multi-step plan noted.
- notes/todo.md: #1 updated (finalize done; remaining nine →
  0.47.0-N); new "Migration B: finalize flags → options_flags"
  item; Done entry; [86] ref.
- notes/chores-09.md: 0.46.0 subsection.
```
